===== source/curated_artifact.py | SHA256 e65e7de2b26b00f7ed6a174d02eeb51c9efa7b42793f33875f89e7871c85ee06 =====
0001: """WP-R5-004-ISOLATED-INTEGRATION: curated standalone TRAIN-only artifact authorization, EOF loaders and prepare().
0002: 
0003: Director DECISION: a curated standalone TRAIN-only artifact may end exactly at EOF; no out-of-window terminal sentinel row is
0004: required for THIS artifact schema. The original raw-file interval schema (``WP004_REAL_EXECUTION_AUTHORIZATION_V1``) is untouched
0005: and keeps refusing exactly as before; V1 byte offsets are never reinterpreted as artifact offsets. This module has its own
0006: schema (``WP004_CURATED_ARTIFACT_AUTHORIZATION_V1``) that describes artifacts only by alias, full-file sha256, size, row count and
0007: declared timestamp extent. The artifact commitment is CURRENT-ACQUISITION IDENTITY, not historical sealed membership.
0008: 
0009: Every byte of an artifact is approved (the whole file's sha256 is pinned) and every byte is consumed and validated: header,
0010: declared extent, row count, strictly increasing timestamps, TRAIN window, warm-up-only no-fit rows, session set. Any violation
0011: fails closed. ``validate_curated_authorization`` is pure and opens no data file.
0012: 
0013: Engine consequence of an EOF artifact (disclosed, no value is changed): the engine's trading clock never includes the LAST bar
0014: of a frame (``build_clock`` iterates ``range(warmup, len-1)``). Without a sentinel the last TRAIN row of the artifact is that
0015: structural terminal bar; it is part of the frame but is never a trading-clock event.
0016: """

0131: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- metadata manifests (pure)
0132: def parse_manifests(stock_bytes: bytes, grid_bytes: bytes, protocol: dict):
0133:     """Pinned stock/grid metadata manifests -> (sorted stock symbols, sorted grid feed names). Opens no market data."""
0134:     try:
0135:         stock, grid = json.loads(stock_bytes), json.loads(grid_bytes)
0136:         files, mhash = stock["files"], stock["manifest_hash"]
0137:         pinned = protocol["stock_dataset"]["manifest_hash"]
0138:         symbols = [f["symbol"] for f in files]
0139:         entries = grid["files"]
0140:         names = [e["name"] for e in entries]
0141:         sealed = protocol["grid_dataset"]["feeds"]
0142:     except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
0143:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"manifest metadata malformed: {exc!r}") from None
0144:     if not (h.is_sha256(pinned) and h.is_sha256(mhash)) or mhash != pinned:
0145:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("stock manifest identity differs from the protocol pin")
0146:     if h.sha256_bytes(json.dumps(files, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode()) != pinned:
0147:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("stock manifest records do not reproduce the pinned manifest hash")
0148:     if len(set(symbols)) != len(symbols) or stock.get("symbol_count") != len(symbols):
0149:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("stock manifest symbol set inconsistent")
0150:     if "symbols" in protocol["stock_dataset"] and int(protocol["stock_dataset"]["symbols"]) != len(symbols):
0151:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("stock manifest symbol count differs from protocol")
0152:     if len(set(names)) != len(names) or set(names) != set(sealed):
0153:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("grid feed set differs from the sealed protocol")
0154:     for entry in entries:
0155:         if not (h.is_sha256(entry.get("sha256")) and entry["sha256"] == sealed[entry["name"]].get("sha256")):
0156:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"{entry['name']}: grid manifest sha256 differs from the sealed protocol")
0157:     return sorted(symbols), sorted(names)

0161: def validate_curated_authorization(auth_bytes: bytes, *, authorization_sha256_pin, protocol, bindings,
0162:                                    stock_symbols, grid_feeds) -> CuratedPlan:
0163:     """Pure validation of the whole curated-artifact authorization against independently supplied ``bindings`` (never read from
0164:     the authorization). Opens NO data file. Unknown/missing/different anything refuses; there is no override key and no
0165:     release field: release is a separate externally pinned record (``release_record``)."""
0166:     if not h.is_sha256(authorization_sha256_pin):
0167:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("malformed authorization sha256 pin")
0168:     actual_sha = h.sha256_bytes(auth_bytes)
0169:     if actual_sha != authorization_sha256_pin:
0170:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("authorization file sha256 differs from pin")
0171:     if set(bindings) != set(BINDING_KEYS):
0172:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"bindings must be exactly {sorted(BINDING_KEYS)}")
0173:     try:
0174:         auth = json.loads(auth_bytes)
0175:     except ValueError as exc:
0176:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"authorization is not valid JSON: {exc}") from None
0177:     _exact(auth, _TOP, "authorization")
0178:     if auth["schema"] != CURATED_AUTH_SCHEMA:
0179:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"authorization schema != {CURATED_AUTH_SCHEMA}")
0180:     for key, value in (("contract_id", h.CONTRACT_ID), ("risk_scope", h.RISK_SCOPE), ("cnc_runtime", h.CNC_STATE),
0181:                        ("commitment_scope", h.COMMITMENT_SCOPE)):
0182:         if auth[key] != value:
0183:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"authorization {key} != required {value!r}")
0184:     classification = auth["classification"]
0185:     if CLASSIFICATION_ORIGIN.get(classification) is None or auth["data_origin"] != CLASSIFICATION_ORIGIN[classification]:
0186:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"classification/data_origin pair not recognised: {classification!r}/{auth['data_origin']!r}")
0187:     execution_id = _text(auth["execution_id"], "execution_id")
0188:     if execution_id != bindings["execution_id"]:
0189:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("execution_id differs from the supplied execution identity")
0190:     _text(auth["execution_package_id"], "execution_package_id")
0191:     if auth["pretrain_policy"] not in h.PRETRAIN_POLICIES:
0192:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("pretrain_policy must be REJECT or WARMUP_PROVENANCE_NO_FIT")
0193:     prov = _exact(auth["provenance"], _PROV, "provenance")
0194:     for key in ("issuer", "approval_id", "derivation_id"):
0195:         _text(prov[key], f"provenance.{key}")
0196:     if not h.is_sha256(prov["approval_record_sha256"]):
0197:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("provenance.approval_record_sha256 malformed")
0198:     train = _exact(auth["train"], {"train_start", "train_end_exclusive"}, "train")
0199:     periods = protocol["periods"]
0200:     if train["train_start"] != periods["train_start"] or train["train_end_exclusive"] != periods["train_end_exclusive"]:
0201:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("TRAIN boundaries differ from the protocol periods")
0202:     stage = _exact(auth["stage_b"], {"block", "sessions"}, "stage_b")
0203:     declared = protocol["sampling_plan"]["stage_b"][0]
0204:     if type(stage["block"]) is not int or stage["block"] != 1 or int(declared["block"]) != 1 \
0205:             or stage["sessions"] != list(declared["sessions"]):
0206:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("stage_b block/sessions differ from protocol Stage B Block 1")
0207:     pins = _exact(auth["pins"], _PINS, "pins")
0208:     for key in _PINS - {"snapshot_head", "os_isolation_profile"}:
0209:         if not h.is_sha256(pins[key]) or pins[key] != bindings[key]:
0210:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"pin {key} differs from the supplied value")
0211:     if pins["snapshot_head"] != h.EXPECTED_HEAD:
0212:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("snapshot_head differs from harness EXPECTED_HEAD")
0213:     if pins["os_isolation_profile"] != bindings["os_isolation_profile"]:
0214:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("os_isolation_profile differs from the supplied profile")
0215:     out = _exact(auth["output"], _OUT, "output")
0216:     for key in _OUT:
0217:         if out[key] != bindings[key] or not isinstance(out[key], str) or not Path(out[key]).is_absolute() \
0218:                 or ".." in Path(out[key]).parts:
0219:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"output.{key} differs from the supplied output identity")
0220:     if Path(out["output_dir"]) == Path(out["output_root"]) or not Path(out["output_dir"]).is_relative_to(out["output_root"]):
0221:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("output_dir must lie strictly inside output_root")
0222:     entries = auth["artifacts"]
0223:     if not isinstance(entries, list) or not entries:
0224:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError("artifacts must be a non-empty list")
0225:     artifacts, seen_names = {}, set()
0226:     reserved = set(META_ALIASES.values())
0227:     for entry in entries:
0228:         _exact(entry, _ART, "artifact entry")
0229:         role, name, alias = entry["role"], _text(entry["name"], "artifact name"), entry["alias"]
0230:         if role not in ("stock", "grid"):
0231:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"{name}: role must be 'stock' or 'grid'")
0232:         if not isinstance(alias, str) or not _ALIAS.fullmatch(alias) or alias in reserved or alias in artifacts:
0233:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"{name}: bad, reserved or duplicate alias {alias!r}")
0234:         if (role, name) in seen_names:
0235:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"duplicate artifact {role}/{name}")
0236:         seen_names.add((role, name))
0237:         if not h.is_sha256(entry["sha256"]):
0238:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"{name}: sha256 malformed")
0239:         if entry["ends_at_eof"] is not True:
0240:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"{name}: ends_at_eof must be true (whole file approved, bytes end at EOF)")
0241:         pre = _count(entry["pretrain_rows"], f"{name}: pretrain_rows", minimum=0)
0242:         if pre and (role != "stock" or auth["pretrain_policy"] != "WARMUP_PROVENANCE_NO_FIT"):
0243:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError(f"{name}: preTRAIN rows only as stock WARMUP_PROVENANCE_NO_FIT")
0244:         artifacts[alias] = CuratedArtifact(
0245:             role=role, name=name, alias=alias, sha256=entry["sha256"], size_bytes=_count(entry["size_bytes"], f"{name}: size_bytes"),
0246:             row_count=_count(entry["row_count"], f"{name}: row_count"),
0247:             first_timestamp=_text(entry["first_timestamp"], f"{name}: first_timestamp"),
0248:             last_timestamp=_text(entry["last_timestamp"], f"{name}: last_timestamp"), pretrain_rows=pre,
0249:             provenance_id=_text(entry["provenance_id"], f"{name}: provenance_id"))
0250:     stock = {a.name for a in artifacts.values() if a.role == "stock"}
0251:     grid = {a.name for a in artifacts.values() if a.role == "grid"}
0252:     if stock != set(stock_symbols) or grid != set(grid_feeds):
0253:         raise CuratedAuthorizationError(
0254:             f"symbol/grid bijection failed: stock missing={sorted(set(stock_symbols) - stock)[:5]} "
0255:             f"extra={sorted(stock - set(stock_symbols))[:5]}; grid missing={sorted(set(grid_feeds) - grid)[:5]} "
0256:             f"extra={sorted(grid - set(grid_feeds))[:5]}")
0257:     return CuratedPlan(authorization_sha256=actual_sha, execution_id=execution_id,
0258:                        execution_package_id=auth["execution_package_id"], classification=classification,
0259:                        data_origin=auth["data_origin"], pretrain_policy=auth["pretrain_policy"],
0260:                        train_start=train["train_start"], train_end_exclusive=train["train_end_exclusive"],
0261:                        sessions=tuple(stage["sessions"]), provenance=dict(prov), pins=dict(pins), output=dict(out),
0262:                        artifacts=artifacts)

0265: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- loaders (EOF artifacts)
0266: def _open_reader(path, art: CuratedArtifact, chunk_size):
0267:     if os.stat(path).st_size != art.size_bytes:
0268:         raise CuratedArtifactError(f"{art.alias}: size differs from the authorization")
0269:     ARTIFACT_OPENS.append(art.alias)
0270:     # cap = size + 1: the extra byte only allows the zero-length read that proves EOF; growth past the size fails closed below
0271:     return h.BoundedLineReader(path, chunk_size=chunk_size, max_physical_bytes=art.size_bytes + 1,
0272:                                expected_commitment=art.sha256)
0273: 
0274: 
0275: def _finish_reader(reader, art: CuratedArtifact, data_rows, first_raw, last_raw):
0276:     if reader.rows - 1 != data_rows:
0277:         raise h.ShapeError(f"{art.alias}: {reader.rows - 1} data lines but {data_rows} parsed rows (blank/unparsed lines)")
0278:     if not reader.reached_eof or reader.physical_bytes_read != art.size_bytes or reader.consumed_bytes != art.size_bytes:
0279:         raise CuratedArtifactError(f"{art.alias}: artifact not consumed exactly to EOF")
0280:     ledger_row = reader.record(strict_prefix=False)         # also verifies consumed bytes == full-file sha256 commitment
0281:     if data_rows != art.row_count:
0282:         raise CuratedArtifactError(f"{art.alias}: row count {data_rows} != authorized {art.row_count}")
0283:     if first_raw != art.first_timestamp or last_raw != art.last_timestamp:
0284:         raise CuratedArtifactError(f"{art.alias}: timestamp extent {first_raw!r}..{last_raw!r} != authorized "
0285:                                    f"{art.first_timestamp!r}..{art.last_timestamp!r}")
0286:     return ledger_row
0287: 
0288: 
0289: def load_curated_stock(path, art: CuratedArtifact, sessions, warmup_bars, *, train_start, train_end_exclusive,
0290:                        pretrain_policy, chunk_size=h.DEFAULT_CHUNK):
0291:     """Whole artifact, EOF-terminated. Returns (frame, audit, ledger_row). The last row is the engine terminal bar."""
0292:     import pandas as pd
0293:     w = h._worker()
0294:     target_dates = [pd.Timestamp(x).date() for x in sessions]
0295:     target_set = set(target_dates)
0296:     first_date, last_date = target_dates[0], target_dates[-1]
0297:     end_date, start_date = pd.Timestamp(train_end_exclusive).date(), pd.Timestamp(train_start).date()
0298:     if last_date >= end_date:
0299:         raise h.ChronologyError(f"{art.alias}: block extends to/after TRAIN end {end_date}")
0300:     warmup, target, seen, pretrain_rows, previous = deque(maxlen=warmup_bars), [], set(), 0, None
0301:     first_raw = last_raw = None
0302:     started, parsed = False, 0
0303:     reader = _open_reader(path, art, chunk_size)
0304:     try:
0305:         rows = h._dict_reader(reader, art.alias, ["open", "high", "low", "close", "volume"])
0306:         fields = rows.fieldnames
0307:         ts_column = "timestamp" if "timestamp" in fields else "date" if "date" in fields else None
0308:         if ts_column is None:
0309:             raise h.ShapeError(f"{art.alias}: no timestamp/date column")
0310:         for row in rows:
0311:             h._checked(row, art.alias)
0312:             parsed += 1
0313:             ts = w.exchange_ts(row[ts_column])
0314:             first_raw = row[ts_column] if first_raw is None else first_raw
0315:             last_raw = row[ts_column]
0316:             if previous is not None and ts <= previous:
0317:                 raise h.ChronologyError(f"{art.alias}: timestamps not strictly increasing at {ts}")
0318:             previous = ts
0319:             day = ts.date()
0320:             if day >= end_date:
0321:                 raise h.ChronologyError(f"{art.alias}: row {day} at/after TRAIN end {end_date}")
0322:             if day < start_date:
0323:                 if pretrain_policy != "WARMUP_PROVENANCE_NO_FIT":
0324:                     raise h.PreTrainSelectionError(f"{art.alias}: preTRAIN row {day} present but not authorized")
0325:                 if day >= first_date:
0326:                     raise h.PreTrainSelectionError(f"{art.alias}: preTRAIN row {day} is not warmup")
0327:                 pretrain_rows += 1
0328:             if not started:
0329:                 if day < first_date:
0330:                     warmup.append(row)
0331:                     continue
0332:                 if day > first_date:
0333:                     raise ValueError(f"{art.alias}: first target session missing {first_date}")
0334:                 started = True
0335:             if day > last_date:
0336:                 raise CuratedArtifactError(f"{art.alias}: row {day} after the last block session {last_date} "
0337:                                            "(the artifact must end with the block)")
0338:             if day not in target_set:
0339:                 raise ValueError(f"{art.alias}: unexpected session {day} inside declared block")
0340:             target.append(row)
0341:             seen.add(day)
0342:         ledger_row = _finish_reader(reader, art, parsed, first_raw, last_raw)
0343:     finally:
0344:         reader.close()
0345:     if pretrain_rows != art.pretrain_rows:
0346:         raise CuratedArtifactError(f"{art.alias}: preTRAIN rows {pretrain_rows} != authorized {art.pretrain_rows}")
0347:     if len(warmup) != warmup_bars:
0348:         raise ValueError(f"{art.alias}: expected {warmup_bars} warmup bars, got {len(warmup)}")
0349:     if seen != target_set:
0350:         raise ValueError(f"{art.alias}: missing target sessions {sorted(target_set - seen)}")
0351:     if len(target) < 2:
0352:         raise ValueError(f"{art.alias}: fewer than two block rows (no trading clock event possible)")
0353:     frame = w.convert_stock_rows(list(warmup) + target)
0354:     warm_frame, target_frame = frame.iloc[:warmup_bars], frame.iloc[warmup_bars:]
0355:     counts = {}
0356:     for ts in target_frame["timestamp"]:
0357:         key = str(w.exchange_ts(ts).date())
0358:         counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
0359:     audit = {
0360:         "warmup_rows": len(warm_frame), "warmup_first_utc": w.utc_iso(warm_frame["timestamp"].iloc[0]),
0361:         "warmup_last_utc": w.utc_iso(warm_frame["timestamp"].iloc[-1]), "target_rows": len(target_frame),
0362:         "clock_rows": len(target_frame) - 1, "target_first_utc": w.utc_iso(target_frame["timestamp"].iloc[0]),
0363:         "target_last_utc": w.utc_iso(target_frame["timestamp"].iloc[-1]), "target_session_counts": counts,
0364:         "terminal_bar_policy": TERMINAL_BAR_POLICY, "terminal_sentinel_row_present": False, "ends_at_eof": True,
0365:         "artifact_rows": reader.rows - 1, "artifact_pretrain_rows": pretrain_rows,
0366:         "consumed_prefix_sha256": ledger_row["consumed_prefix_sha256"], "consumed_bytes": ledger_row["consumed_bytes"],
0367:         "physical_bytes_read": ledger_row["physical_bytes_read"], "pretrain_policy": pretrain_policy,
0368:         "warmup_rows_dated_before_train_start": sum(1 for r in warmup if w.exchange_ts(r[ts_column]).date() < start_date),
0369:         "train_scope": h.TRAIN_SCOPE_DISCLOSURE,
0370:     }
0371:     return frame, audit, ledger_row
0372: 
0373: 
0374: def load_curated_grid(path, art: CuratedArtifact, name, protocol, cutoff_utc, *, chunk_size=h.DEFAULT_CHUNK):
0375:     """Whole grid artifact validated to EOF; rows available after ``cutoff_utc`` are validated then discarded, never retained."""
0376:     import pandas as pd
0377:     sealed = protocol["grid_dataset"]["feeds"][name]
0378:     delay = int(protocol["grid_dataset"]["availability_delay_minutes"])
0379:     start_date, end_date = h._train_bounds(protocol)
0380:     ts_column = sealed["timestamp_column"]
0381:     rows, previous, total, discarded, first_raw, last_raw = [], None, 0, 0, None, None
0382:     reader = _open_reader(path, art, chunk_size)
0383:     try:
0384:         for row in h._dict_reader(reader, name, [ts_column, "close"]):
0385:             h._checked(row, name)
0386:             source = pd.to_datetime(row[ts_column], utc=True)
0387:             local = source.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata").date()
0388:             if local >= end_date:
0389:                 raise h.ChronologyError(f"{name}: grid row {source} at/after TRAIN end {end_date}")
0390:             if local < start_date:
0391:                 raise h.PreTrainSelectionError(f"{name}: preTRAIN grid row {source} in a grid artifact")
0392:             availability = source + pd.Timedelta(minutes=delay)
0393:             if previous is not None and availability <= previous:
0394:                 raise h.ChronologyError(f"{name}: grid timestamps not strictly increasing at {availability}")
0395:             previous = availability
0396:             first_raw = row[ts_column] if first_raw is None else first_raw
0397:             last_raw = row[ts_column]
0398:             total += 1
0399:             try:
0400:                 float(row["close"])
0401:             except ValueError:
0402:                 raise h.ShapeError(f"{name}: non-numeric close in row {reader.rows}") from None
0403:             if availability > cutoff_utc:
0404:                 discarded += 1
0405:                 continue
0406:             rows.append({"timestamp": availability, "close": row["close"]})
0407:         ledger_row = _finish_reader(reader, art, total, first_raw, last_raw)
0408:     finally:
0409:         reader.close()
0410:     if not rows:
0411:         raise ValueError(f"{name}: no causal grid prefix")
0412:     frame = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0413:     frame["timestamp"] = pd.to_datetime(frame["timestamp"], utc=True)
0414:     frame["close"] = pd.to_numeric(frame["close"], errors="raise")
0415:     if (frame["timestamp"] > cutoff_utc).any():
0416:         raise ValueError(f"{name}: post-cutoff row retained")
0417:     audit = {
0418:         "declared_sha256_unverified_metadata": sealed["sha256"], "consumed_prefix_sha256": ledger_row["consumed_prefix_sha256"],
0419:         "consumed_bytes": ledger_row["consumed_bytes"], "physical_bytes_read": ledger_row["physical_bytes_read"],
0420:         "artifact_rows": total, "rows": len(frame), "first_available_utc": frame["timestamp"].iloc[0].isoformat(),
0421:         "last_available_utc": frame["timestamp"].iloc[-1].isoformat(), "cutoff_utc": cutoff_utc.isoformat(),
0422:         "rows_after_cutoff_retained": 0, "rows_after_cutoff_validated_and_discarded": discarded, "ends_at_eof": True,
0423:         "train_scope": h.TRAIN_SCOPE_DISCLOSURE,
0424:     }
0425:     return frame, audit, ledger_row
0426: 
0427:

0429: @dataclass
0430: class CuratedContext:
0431:     plan: CuratedPlan
0432:     paths: dict            # alias -> absolute path of the artifact as visible to the process (/in/<alias> in the sandbox)
0433:     root: str              # the "root" the factory passes to prepare; must match (metadata root, never a data root)
0434:     stock_symbols: tuple
0435:     grid_feeds: tuple

0438: 
0439: 
0440: def make_curated_prepare(ctx: CuratedContext, ledger: h.ReadLedger):
0441:     """prepare(root, protocol, block) -> (frames, feeds, audit): drop-in for the factory's explicit ``prepare`` argument."""
0442:     plan = ctx.plan
0443: 
0444:     def prepare(root, protocol, block):
0445:         import pandas as pd
0446:         w = h._worker()

0449:         if str(root) != ctx.root or list(plan.sessions) != sessions:
0450:             raise h.UnsafeRootError(f"prepare root/sessions differ from the authorized plan (root={root})")
0451:         periods = protocol["periods"]
0452:         if periods["train_start"] != plan.train_start or periods["train_end_exclusive"] != plan.train_end_exclusive:
0453:             raise h.ChronologyError("protocol TRAIN boundaries differ from the authorization")

0456:             raise CuratedAuthorizationError("artifact set differs from the symbol/grid bijection")
0457:         # ---- PREFLIGHT: every artifact is stat'ed (size) before ANY artifact is opened

0460:             if not os.path.isabs(path) or os.path.islink(path) or not os.path.isfile(path) or os.stat(path).st_size != art.size_bytes:
0461:                 raise CuratedArtifactError(f"{art.alias}: not a plain file of the authorized size")

0463:         frames, stock_audit = {}, {}

0470:             frames[symbol], stock_audit[symbol] = frame, audit
0471:         certified = Revision2ExternalEngineOrchestrator.prepare_market_data(frames)
0472:         clock = Revision2ExternalEngineOrchestrator.build_clock(certified, warmup_bars)
0473:         if len(clock) != sum(a["clock_rows"] for a in stock_audit.values()):
0474:             raise ValueError(f"clock event mismatch: {len(clock)}")
0475:         target_dates = {pd.Timestamp(x).date() for x in sessions}
0476:         for event in clock:
0477:             if w.exchange_ts(event.timestamp).date() not in target_dates:
0478:                 raise ValueError("clock escaped target sessions")
0479:             if event.bar_idx >= len(certified[event.symbol]) - 1:
0480:                 raise ValueError("terminal bar entered trading clock")
0481:         max_target_utc = max(w.exchange_ts(f["timestamp"].iloc[-2]).tz_convert("UTC") for f in certified.values())
0482:         feeds, grid_audit = {}, {}

0486:                                                                    chunk_size=ctx.chunk_size)

0490:             "sessions": sessions, "warmup_bars_per_symbol": warmup_bars, "stock": stock_audit, "clock_events": len(clock),
0491:             "grid": grid_audit, "max_target_utc": max_target_utc.isoformat(), "terminal_bar_policy": TERMINAL_BAR_POLICY,
0492:         }
0493:         canonical["slice_sha256"] = w.canonical_json_hash(canonical)

0496:     return prepare

0499: # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- source-root audit
0500: def _under(path, roots):
0501:     real = os.path.realpath(path)
0502:     return any(real == r or real.startswith(r.rstrip("/") + "/") for r in roots)
0503: 
0504: 
0505: def audit_source_roots(allowed_roots) -> dict:
0506:     """Every sys.path entry and every imported module/package origin must lie under the pinned mounts (code roots + runtime)."""
0507:     roots = tuple(os.path.realpath(r) for r in allowed_roots)
0508:     bad_path = [p for p in sys.path if not _under(p or os.getcwd(), roots)]
0509:     bad_modules = []
0510:     for name, module in list(sys.modules.items()):
0511:         origins = [getattr(module, "__file__", None), *list(getattr(module, "__path__", None) or [])]
0512:         bad_modules += [f"{name}:{o}" for o in origins if o and not _under(o, roots)]
0513:     if bad_path or bad_modules:
0514:         raise SourceRootEscape(f"sources outside the pinned mounts: sys.path={bad_path[:5]} modules={sorted(bad_modules)[:5]}")
0515:     return dict(allowed_roots=list(roots), sys_path=list(sys.path),
0516:                 module_origins=sum(1 for m in sys.modules.values() if getattr(m, "__file__", None)))

===== source/release_record.py | SHA256 2e6149413ce0b079febb6a4d7e8355f67272d1f07a70bc6b0e78f9ecb031aedc =====
0001: """WP-R5-004-ISOLATED-INTEGRATION: release record validator (pure; opens no data).
0002: 
0003: A release record is a Director-issued JSON document that binds ONE release to the exact isolation profile, code-tree pins,
0004: input pins, execution id and curated-artifact authorization sha256. It is accepted only when ALL of these hold:
0005: 
0006: * its sha256 equals an EXTERNAL pin handed in by the caller (command line / operator / ``os_isolation`` constant). A pin
0007:   stored in the record, in the authorization or next to them is not external and is never read;
0008: * its issuer is trusted for the record's scope by an EXTERNAL issuer table (SYNTHETIC_ONLY: caller-supplied fixture issuers;
0009:   REAL_TRAIN_DIAGNOSTIC: ``REAL_TRUSTED_ISSUERS`` below, which is EMPTY, and ``os_isolation.REAL_RUN_RELEASE_ACCEPTANCE_SHA256``,
0010:   which is ``None``: the real release is closed and no code path in this module opens it);
0011: * every binding equals the live, independently derived value (``expected``);
0012: * the classification matches the scope: a SYNTHETIC_ONLY grant can never authorize a real-data classification.
0013: 
0014: There is no flag, no self-pinned JSON and no authorization field that grants release.
0015: """
0016: from __future__ import annotations
0017: 
0018: import hashlib
0019: import json
0020: import re
0021: from dataclasses import dataclass
0022: 
0023: SCHEMA = "WP004_RELEASE_RECORD_V1"
0024: SCOPE_SYNTHETIC = "SYNTHETIC_ONLY"
0025: SCOPE_REAL = "REAL_TRAIN_DIAGNOSTIC"
0026: SYNTHETIC_CLASSIFICATION = "SYNTHETIC_INTEGRATION_ONLY"
0027: REAL_CLASSIFICATION = "NON_SEALED_TRAIN_EXECUTION_DIAGNOSTIC"
0028: SCOPE_FOR_CLASSIFICATION = {SYNTHETIC_CLASSIFICATION: SCOPE_SYNTHETIC, REAL_CLASSIFICATION: SCOPE_REAL}
0029: REAL_TRUSTED_ISSUERS: dict = {}      # issuer -> scopes. EMPTY: no Director-approved real issuer exists. Not configurable at runtime.
0030: _KEYS = {"schema", "record_id", "issuer", "release_scope", "isolation_profile", "classification", "execution_id",
0031:          "authorization_sha256", "code_pins", "input_pins"}
0032: _HEX = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}")
0033: 
0034: 
0035: class ReleaseError(Exception):
0036:     pass
0037: 
0038: 
0039: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0040: class ReleaseGrant:
0041:     scope: str
0042:     record_id: str
0043:     issuer: str
0044:     record_sha256: str
0045:     classification: str
0046:     execution_id: str
0047:     authorization_sha256: str
0048: 
0049:     @property
0050:     def authorizes_real_classification(self) -> bool:
0051:         return self.scope == SCOPE_REAL
0052: 
0053: 
0054: def _is_sha(value) -> bool:
0055:     return isinstance(value, str) and bool(_HEX.fullmatch(value))
0056: 
0057: 
0058: def _real_pin():
0059:     import os_isolation as osi
0060:     return osi.REAL_RUN_RELEASE_ACCEPTANCE_SHA256
0061: 
0062: 
0063: def validate_release_record(record_bytes: bytes, *, external_pin, trusted_issuers, expected: dict) -> ReleaseGrant:
0064:     """``external_pin``: sha256 supplied out of band. ``trusted_issuers``: {issuer: [scopes]} (only SYNTHETIC_ONLY entries
0065:     are honoured). ``expected``: isolation_profile, classification, execution_id, authorization_sha256, code_pins, input_pins
0066:     derived by the caller from the live/verified state (never from the record)."""
0067:     if not _is_sha(external_pin):
0068:         raise ReleaseError("release record: external pin missing or malformed (no self-pinned release)")
0069:     actual = hashlib.sha256(record_bytes).hexdigest()
0070:     if actual != external_pin:
0071:         raise ReleaseError("release record sha256 differs from the external pin")
0072:     try:
0073:         rec = json.loads(record_bytes)
0074:     except ValueError as exc:
0075:         raise ReleaseError(f"release record is not valid JSON: {exc}") from None
0076:     if not isinstance(rec, dict) or set(rec) != _KEYS:
0077:         raise ReleaseError(f"release record keys must be exactly {sorted(_KEYS)}")
0078:     if rec["schema"] != SCHEMA:
0079:         raise ReleaseError("release record schema mismatch")
0080:     for key in ("record_id", "issuer", "execution_id", "isolation_profile"):
0081:         if not isinstance(rec[key], str) or not rec[key].strip():
0082:             raise ReleaseError(f"release record {key} missing")
0083:     scope = rec["release_scope"]
0084:     if scope not in (SCOPE_SYNTHETIC, SCOPE_REAL):
0085:         raise ReleaseError(f"unknown release scope {scope!r}")
0086:     if SCOPE_FOR_CLASSIFICATION.get(rec["classification"]) != scope:
0087:         raise ReleaseError(f"release scope {scope} does not match classification {rec['classification']!r}")
0088:     if scope == SCOPE_REAL:
0089:         if not REAL_TRUSTED_ISSUERS or rec["issuer"] not in REAL_TRUSTED_ISSUERS or _real_pin() != actual:
0090:             raise ReleaseError("real-data release closed: no Director-approved real issuer / "
0091:                                "REAL_RUN_RELEASE_ACCEPTANCE_SHA256 pin exists for this record")
0092:     else:
0093:         scopes = (trusted_issuers or {}).get(rec["issuer"])
0094:         if not scopes or scope not in scopes:
0095:             raise ReleaseError(f"issuer {rec['issuer']!r} is not externally trusted for scope {scope}")
0096:     if not _is_sha(rec["authorization_sha256"]):
0097:         raise ReleaseError("release record authorization_sha256 malformed")
0098:     for key in ("isolation_profile", "classification", "execution_id", "authorization_sha256"):
0099:         if rec[key] != expected[key]:
0100:             raise ReleaseError(f"release record {key} differs from the live value")
0101:     for key in ("code_pins", "input_pins"):
0102:         pins = rec[key]
0103:         if not isinstance(pins, dict) or not pins or not all(_is_sha(v) for v in pins.values()):
0104:             raise ReleaseError(f"release record {key} malformed")
0105:         if pins != dict(expected[key]):
0106:             raise ReleaseError(f"release record {key} differ from the live pins (exact set and values required)")
0107:     return ReleaseGrant(scope=scope, record_id=rec["record_id"], issuer=rec["issuer"], record_sha256=actual,
0108:                         classification=rec["classification"], execution_id=rec["execution_id"],
0109:                         authorization_sha256=rec["authorization_sha256"])

0001: #!/usr/bin/env python3
0002: """Minimal HOST launcher for the WP-R5-004 isolated integration: validates everything that can be validated from metadata
0003: alone, then starts ``curated_target.py`` through ``os_isolation.launch`` (bwrap).
0004: 
0005:     /home/srinivas/.venvs/zerodha-phase1-r5/bin/python -I curated_launcher.py --config CONFIG.json
0006: 
0007: Host-side order (no artifact byte is opened before step 4; an unknown or invalid authorization or release record stops here):
0008:  1. read ONLY metadata files (authorization, release record, protocol, params, manifests, contract, snapshot manifest);

0010:     sha256 values, OS profile, execution id, output identity) and the pinned authorization sha256;

0001: """WP-R5-004-OS-ISOLATION: minimal filesystem / network / process isolation launcher built on /usr/bin/bwrap.

0007:   libraries reported by ``ldd`` for the interpreter and its extension modules (file binds, never a library directory);

0095: # --------------------------------------------------------------------- hashing
0096: def sha256_file(path) -> str:
0097:     h = hashlib.sha256()
0098:     with open(path, "rb") as f:
0099:         for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
0100:             h.update(chunk)
0101:     return h.hexdigest()
0102: 
0103: 
0104: def tree_sha256(root) -> str:
0105:     """sha256 over sorted (relative path, file sha256) of every regular file; symlinks/specials refuse; __pycache__ ignored."""
0106:     root, rows = Path(root), []
0107:     for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(root, followlinks=False):
0108:         dn[:] = sorted(d for d in dn if d != "__pycache__")
0109:         for name in dn:
0110:             if os.path.islink(os.path.join(dp, name)):
0111:                 raise PinMismatch(f"symlinked directory inside code root: {os.path.join(dp, name)}")
0112:         for name in sorted(fn):
0113:             p = os.path.join(dp, name)
0114:             if not stat.S_ISREG(os.lstat(p).st_mode):
0115:                 raise PinMismatch(f"non-regular file inside code root: {p}")
0116:             rows.append(f"{os.path.relpath(p, root)}\0{sha256_file(p)}\n")
0117:     return hashlib.sha256("".join(rows).encode()).hexdigest()
0118: 
0119: 
0120: # --------------------------------------------------------------------- environment
0121: def check_environment() -> dict:
0122:     """Fail closed (UnsupportedEnvironment) unless the launcher prerequisites are present. Does not launch anything."""
0123:     if not sys.platform.startswith("linux"):
0124:         raise UnsupportedEnvironment("linux required")
0125:     for tool in (BWRAP, LDD):
0126:         if not os.path.isfile(tool) or not os.access(tool, os.X_OK):
0127:             raise UnsupportedEnvironment(f"{tool} missing or not executable")
0128:     if os.stat(BWRAP).st_mode & stat.S_ISUID:
0129:         raise UnsupportedEnvironment("setuid bwrap not supported: the unprivileged user-namespace path is required")
0130:     out = subprocess.run([BWRAP, "--version"], capture_output=True, text=True, env={}, timeout=20)
0131:     m = re.search(r"(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)", out.stdout)
0132:     if out.returncode != 0 or not m or tuple(map(int, m.groups())) < (0, 8, 0):
0133:         raise UnsupportedEnvironment(f"bwrap >= 0.8.0 required (--disable-userns): {out.stdout.strip()!r}")
0134:     for kind in NS_KINDS:
0135:         if not os.path.exists(f"/proc/self/ns/{kind}"):
0136:             raise UnsupportedEnvironment(f"kernel lacks /proc/self/ns/{kind}")
0137:     return dict(bwrap=BWRAP, bwrap_sha256=sha256_file(BWRAP), bwrap_version=out.stdout.strip(), uid=os.getuid(),
0138:                 kernel=os.uname().release)
0139: 
0140: 
0141: def _is_under(path: str, root: str) -> bool:
0142:     return path == root or path.startswith(root.rstrip("/") + "/")
0143: 
0144: 
0145: def _symlinks_on_path(path: str) -> list:
0146:     """Every symlink (as (location, link text)) met while resolving ``path`` (components and final file)."""
0147:     out, pending = [], path
0148:     for _ in range(40):
0149:         parts, cur, hop = Path(pending).parts, "/", False
0150:         for i, part in enumerate(parts[1:], 1):
0151:             nxt = os.path.join(cur, part)
0152:             if os.path.islink(nxt):
0153:                 text = os.readlink(nxt)
0154:                 out.append((nxt, text))
0155:                 tgt = text if os.path.isabs(text) else os.path.join(cur, text)
0156:                 pending, hop = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(tgt, *parts[i + 1:])), True
0157:                 break
0158:             cur = nxt
0159:         if not hop:
0160:             return out
0161:     raise UnsupportedEnvironment(f"symlink chain too deep: {path}")
0162: 
0163: 
0164: @functools.lru_cache(maxsize=8)
0165: def _runtime_layout(python: str):
0166:     """Explicit runtime exposure for ``python``: (prefix dirs, [(real_path, dest)], symlink_pairs, root_symlinks)."""
0167:     if not os.path.isabs(python) or not os.path.exists(python):
0168:         raise UnsupportedEnvironment(f"interpreter must be an existing absolute path: {python}")
0169:     out = subprocess.run([python, "-I", "-c", "import sys,json;print(json.dumps([sys.prefix,sys.base_prefix]))"],
0170:                          capture_output=True, text=True, env={}, timeout=60)
0171:     if out.returncode != 0:
0172:         raise UnsupportedEnvironment(f"interpreter probe failed: {out.stderr.strip()}")
0173:     prefix, base = (os.path.realpath(p) for p in json.loads(out.stdout))
0174:     dirs = sorted({prefix, base})
0175:     real_py = os.path.realpath(python)
0176:     if not any(_is_under(real_py, d) for d in dirs) and not _is_under(real_py, "/usr"):
0177:         raise UnsupportedEnvironment(f"interpreter {real_py} outside its prefix dirs {dirs}")
0178:     elfs = {real_py}
0179:     for d in dirs:
0180:         for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(d):
0181:             elfs.update(os.path.realpath(os.path.join(dp, f)) for f in fn if f.endswith(".so") or ".so." in f)
0182:     libs, links, root_links = {}, {}, {}
0183:     for elf in sorted(elfs):
0184:         r = subprocess.run([LDD, elf], capture_output=True, text=True, env={}, timeout=60)
0185:         if r.returncode != 0 and "not a dynamic executable" not in (r.stdout + r.stderr):
0186:             continue   # non-ELF look-alike (e.g. text); a genuinely unresolved lib is caught below
0187:         for line in r.stdout.splitlines():
0188:             if "not found" in line:
0189:                 if elf == real_py:
0190:                     raise UnsupportedEnvironment(f"unresolved shared library for the interpreter: {line.strip()}")
0191:                 continue    # optional extension (e.g. _tkinter) with a missing host lib: simply not importable in the sandbox
0192:             m = re.search(r"(?:=>\s*)?(/\S+)\s+\(0x", line)
0193:             if not m:
0194:                 continue
0195:             p = m.group(1)
0196:             real = os.path.realpath(p)
0197:             if any(_is_under(real, d) for d in dirs):
0198:                 continue
0199:             libs[real] = real
0200:             dest = os.path.join(os.path.realpath(os.path.dirname(p)), os.path.basename(p))
0201:             if dest != real:
0202:                 links[dest] = real
0203:             top = "/" + p.split("/")[1]
0204:             if os.path.islink(top):
0205:                 root_links[top] = os.readlink(top)
0206:     for loc, text in _symlinks_on_path(python):      # e.g. venv bin/python -> uv alias dir -> real interpreter dir
0207:         if not any(_is_under(loc, d) for d in dirs):
0208:             root_links[loc] = text
0209:     return tuple(dirs), tuple(sorted(libs.items())), tuple(sorted(links.items())), tuple(sorted(root_links.items()))
0210: 
0211: 
0212: # --------------------------------------------------------------------- spec / plan
0213: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0214: class InputArtifact:
0215:     host_path: str       # approved standalone TRAIN-only artifact (a FILE; its directory is never mounted)
0216:     alias: str           # mounted as /in/<alias>
0217:     sha256: str
0218:     approval_id: str     # identifier of the external approval of this artifact (recorded in the witness; not verified here)
0219: 
0220: 
0221: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0222: class IsolationSpec:
0223:     python: str
0224:     code_roots: dict                  # name -> host dir (read-only at /code/<name>)
0225:     code_pins: dict                   # name -> tree sha256 (must cover every code root)
0226:     target: str                       # script path relative to /code, e.g. "harness/probe.py"
0227:     target_args: tuple = ()
0228:     inputs: tuple = ()
0229:     output_dir: str = ""
0230:     protected_paths: tuple = ()       # secret / original-repo / raw-data trees: never bound, contained or exposed
0231:     no_expose_paths: tuple = ()       # directories whose own listing must stay hidden (e.g. project root); children may bind
0232:     bootstrap_root: str = "harness"   # code root holding os_isolation.py + os_isolation_bootstrap.py
0233:     timeout_s: float = 120.0
0234: 
0235: 
0236: def _real(path) -> str:
0237:     return os.path.realpath(path)
0238: 
0239: 
0240: def _check_not_broad(src: str, label: str, protected, no_expose):
0241:     """``protected``: trees that may be neither bound, contained in a bind, nor exposed by an ancestor bind (secrets,
0242:     original repos, raw data roots). ``no_expose``: directories that must not be bound or have an ancestor-or-equal bound
0243:     (home, an input's parent dir, the project root), but whose explicitly listed descendants may be."""
0244:     if src in BROAD_DENY:
0245:         raise BroadMountError(f"{label}: broad mount source refused: {src}")
0246:     for p in protected:
0247:         if p == src or _is_under(p, src) or _is_under(src, p):
0248:             raise BroadMountError(f"{label}: {src} overlaps protected path {p} (ancestor/descendant/equal)")
0249:     for p in no_expose:
0250:         if p == src or _is_under(p, src):
0251:             raise BroadMountError(f"{label}: {src} would expose {p}")
0252: 
0253: 
0254: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0255: class Plan:
0256:     spec: IsolationSpec
0257:     runtime_dirs: tuple
0258:     runtime_libs: tuple
0259:     mounts: tuple          # ((kind, src, dest)) for audit/receipt: kind in ro-bind/bind/tmpfs/proc/dev/symlink
0260:     protected: tuple
0261:     no_expose: tuple
0262: 
0263: 
0264: def _protected_sets(spec: IsolationSpec):
0265:     prot = sorted({_real(p) for p in spec.protected_paths})
0266:     exp = {_real(str(Path.home())), "/home", "/root", *(_real(p) for p in spec.no_expose_paths)}
0267:     for a in spec.inputs:
0268:         exp.add(_real(os.path.dirname(a.host_path)))        # an input's parent directory is never mountable
0269:     return prot, sorted(exp)
0270: 
0271: 
0272: def build_plan(spec: IsolationSpec, *, host_precheck: bool = True) -> Plan:
0273:     check_environment()
0274:     if not os.path.isabs(spec.python):
0275:         raise OSIsolationError("python must be absolute")
0276:     protected, no_expose = _protected_sets(spec)
0277:     dirs, libs, links, root_links = _runtime_layout(spec.python)
0278:     mounts = []
0279:     for d in dirs:
0280:         _check_not_broad(d, "runtime dir", protected, no_expose)
0281:         mounts.append(("ro-bind", d, d))
0282:     for real, dest in libs:
0283:         mounts.append(("ro-bind", real, real))
0284:     for dest, real in links:
0285:         mounts.append(("symlink", real, dest))
0286:     for top, target in root_links:
0287:         mounts.append(("symlink", target, top))
0288:     if set(spec.code_roots) != set(spec.code_pins):
0289:         raise PinMismatch("code_pins must cover exactly the code roots")
0290:     if spec.bootstrap_root not in spec.code_roots:
0291:         raise OSIsolationError("bootstrap_root must be one of the code roots")
0292:     for name, host in sorted(spec.code_roots.items()):
0293:         if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", name):
0294:             raise OSIsolationError(f"bad code root name {name!r}")
0295:         src = _real(host)
0296:         if src != str(host) or not os.path.isdir(src) or os.path.islink(host):
0297:             raise OSIsolationError(f"code root must be a real, already-resolved directory: {host}")
0298:         _check_not_broad(src, f"code root {name}", protected, no_expose)
0299:         if host_precheck and tree_sha256(src) != spec.code_pins[name]:
0300:             raise PinMismatch(f"code root {name}: tree sha256 differs from pin")
0301:         mounts.append(("ro-bind", src, f"{CODE_MOUNT}/{name}"))
0302:     for need in ("os_isolation.py", BOOTSTRAP_NAME):
0303:         if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(spec.code_roots[spec.bootstrap_root], need)):
0304:             raise OSIsolationError(f"bootstrap root lacks {need}")
0305:     if not spec.target.startswith(tuple(f"{n}/" for n in spec.code_roots)):
0306:         raise OSIsolationError("target must live under a pinned code root")
0307:     aliases = set()
0308:     for a in spec.inputs:
0309:         if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", a.alias) or a.alias in aliases:
0310:             raise OSIsolationError(f"bad/duplicate input alias {a.alias!r}")
0311:         aliases.add(a.alias)
0312:         if not a.approval_id or not isinstance(a.approval_id, str):
0313:             raise OSIsolationError(f"{a.alias}: approval_id missing")
0314:         if not os.path.isabs(a.host_path) or os.path.islink(a.host_path) or not os.path.isfile(a.host_path):
0315:             raise OSIsolationError(f"{a.alias}: input must be an absolute plain file (no symlink)")
0316:         src = _real(a.host_path)
0317:         if src != a.host_path:
0318:             raise OSIsolationError(f"{a.alias}: input path must be already resolved")
0319:         for p in protected:
0320:             if p == src or _is_under(src, p):
0321:                 raise BroadMountError(f"{a.alias}: input lies inside protected path {p}")
0322:         if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", a.sha256):
0323:             raise PinMismatch(f"{a.alias}: malformed sha256 pin")
0324:         if host_precheck and sha256_file(src) != a.sha256:
0325:             raise PinMismatch(f"{a.alias}: input sha256 differs from pin")
0326:         mounts.append(("ro-bind", src, f"{IN_MOUNT}/{a.alias}"))
0327:     out = spec.output_dir
0328:     if not out or not os.path.isabs(out) or os.path.islink(out) or not os.path.isdir(out) or _real(out) != out:
0329:         raise OSIsolationError("output_dir must be an existing, resolved, non-symlink absolute directory")
0330:     _check_not_broad(out, "output dir", protected, no_expose)
0331:     for name, host in spec.code_roots.items():
0332:         if _is_under(out, host) or _is_under(host, out):
0333:             raise BroadMountError("output dir overlaps a code root (source writes must stay impossible)")
0334:     for a in spec.inputs:
0335:         if _is_under(a.host_path, out):
0336:             raise BroadMountError("output dir contains an input artifact")
0337:     mounts.append(("bind", out, OUT_MOUNT))
0338:     return Plan(spec=spec, runtime_dirs=dirs, runtime_libs=tuple(r for r, _ in libs), mounts=tuple(mounts),
0339:                 protected=tuple(protected), no_expose=tuple(no_expose))
0340: 
0341: 
0342: def build_argv(plan: Plan, info_fd: int) -> list:
0343:     a = [BWRAP, "--unshare-user", "--unshare-pid", "--unshare-net", "--unshare-ipc", "--unshare-uts", "--unshare-cgroup",
0344:          "--disable-userns", "--die-with-parent", "--new-session", "--clearenv", "--cap-drop", "ALL",
0345:          "--hostname", "r5-isolated", "--info-fd", str(info_fd),
0346:          "--proc", "/proc", "--dev", "/dev", "--tmpfs", "/tmp", "--size", str(SCRATCH_BYTES), "--tmpfs", SCRATCH_MOUNT]
0347:     for kind, src, dest in plan.mounts:
0348:         a += [f"--{kind}", src, dest] if kind != "symlink" else ["--symlink", src, dest]
0349:     a += ["--remount-ro", "/",
0350:           "--setenv", "HOME", SCRATCH_MOUNT, "--setenv", "TMPDIR", "/tmp", "--setenv", "LC_ALL", "C.UTF-8",
0351:           "--setenv", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE", "1", "--setenv", "PYTHONNOUSERSITE", "1",
0352:           "--chdir", SCRATCH_MOUNT, "--",
0353:           plan.spec.python, "-I", "-B", f"{CODE_MOUNT}/{plan.spec.bootstrap_root}/{BOOTSTRAP_NAME}"]
0354:     return a
0355: 
0356: 
0357: # bwrap options accepted by the auditor (name -> number of arguments); anything else is refused (fail closed)
0358: _ARITY = {"--unshare-user": 0, "--unshare-pid": 0, "--unshare-net": 0, "--unshare-ipc": 0, "--unshare-uts": 0,
0359:           "--unshare-cgroup": 0, "--disable-userns": 0, "--die-with-parent": 0, "--new-session": 0, "--clearenv": 0,
0360:           "--cap-drop": 1, "--hostname": 1, "--info-fd": 1, "--proc": 1, "--dev": 1, "--tmpfs": 1, "--size": 1,
0361:           "--ro-bind": 2, "--bind": 2, "--symlink": 2, "--remount-ro": 1, "--setenv": 2, "--chdir": 1}
0362: _REQUIRED_FLAGS = ("--unshare-user", "--unshare-pid", "--unshare-net", "--unshare-ipc", "--unshare-uts", "--unshare-cgroup",
0363:                    "--disable-userns", "--die-with-parent", "--new-session", "--clearenv")
0364: 
0365: 
0366: def audit_argv(argv, protected=(), no_expose=()) -> dict:
0367:     """Independent parse of a bwrap argv. Refuses broad/unknown/unsafe mounts and missing isolation flags.
0368:     Returns {'binds': [(kind, src, dest)], 'mount_points': set, 'command': [...]}."""
0369:     if not argv or argv[0] != BWRAP:
0370:         raise BroadMountError("argv[0] must be /usr/bin/bwrap")
0371:     prot = [_real(p) for p in protected]
0372:     seen, binds, mount_points, i = [], [], {"/"}, 1
0373:     while i < len(argv):
0374:         tok = argv[i]
0375:         if tok == "--":
0376:             i += 1
0377:             break
0378:         if tok not in _ARITY:
0379:             raise BroadMountError(f"unknown or forbidden bwrap option: {tok}")
0380:         n = _ARITY[tok]
0381:         args = argv[i + 1:i + 1 + n]
0382:         if len(args) != n:
0383:             raise BroadMountError(f"{tok}: missing arguments")
0384:         seen.append(tok)
0385:         if tok in ("--ro-bind", "--bind"):
0386:             src, dest = args
0387:             if not os.path.isabs(src) or ".." in Path(src).parts or not os.path.isabs(dest):
0388:                 raise BroadMountError(f"relative bind path: {args}")
0389:             if dest == "/" or dest in ("/proc", "/dev", "/tmp", SCRATCH_MOUNT):
0390:                 raise BroadMountError(f"bind over reserved destination {dest}")
0391:             _check_not_broad(_real(src), tok, prot, [_real(p) for p in no_expose])
0392:             if tok == "--bind" and dest != OUT_MOUNT:
0393:                 raise BroadMountError("only the output directory may be writable")
0394:             binds.append((tok[2:], src, dest))
0395:             mount_points.add(dest)
0396:         elif tok in ("--tmpfs", "--proc", "--dev"):
0397:             mount_points.add(args[0])
0398:             if tok == "--dev":
0399:                 mount_points |= DEV_MOUNTS
0400:         elif tok == "--cap-drop" and args != ["ALL"]:
0401:             raise BroadMountError("only --cap-drop ALL is accepted")
0402:         i += 1 + n
0403:     for flag in _REQUIRED_FLAGS:
0404:         if flag not in seen:
0405:             raise BroadMountError(f"required isolation flag missing: {flag}")
0406:     if "--share-net" in argv:
0407:         raise BroadMountError("network must not be shared")
0408:     if not any(argv[j] == "--cap-drop" and argv[j + 1] == "ALL" for j in range(len(argv) - 1)):
0409:         raise BroadMountError("--cap-drop ALL missing")
0410:     return dict(binds=binds, mount_points=mount_points, command=list(argv[i:]))
0411: 
0412: 
0413: # --------------------------------------------------------------------- live probing (host and sandbox side)
0414: def _unescape(s: str) -> str:
0415:     return re.sub(r"\\([0-7]{3})", lambda m: chr(int(m.group(1), 8)), s)
0416: 
0417: 
0418: def parse_mountinfo(text: str) -> list:
0419:     rows = []
0420:     for line in text.splitlines():
0421:         f = line.split()
0422:         if "-" not in f:
0423:             continue
0424:         sep = f.index("-")
0425:         rows.append(dict(mount_point=_unescape(f[4]), options=f[5].split(","), fstype=f[sep + 1]))
0426:     return rows
0427: 
0428: 
0429: def probe_self() -> dict:
0430:     """Live kernel view from inside the process (no trust in any file the launcher supplied)."""
0431:     fds = {}
0432:     for name in os.listdir("/proc/self/fd"):
0433:         try:
0434:             fds[name] = os.readlink(f"/proc/self/fd/{name}")
0435:         except OSError:
0436:             pass   # the listing fd itself
0437:     status = dict(line.split(":\t", 1) for line in Path("/proc/self/status").read_text().splitlines() if ":\t" in line)
0438:     ifaces = [ln.split(":")[0].strip() for ln in Path("/proc/net/dev").read_text().splitlines()[2:]]
0439:     return dict(
0440:         ns={k: os.readlink(f"/proc/self/ns/{k}") for k in NS_KINDS},
0441:         mounts=parse_mountinfo(Path("/proc/self/mountinfo").read_text()),
0442:         root=sorted(os.listdir("/")), fds=fds, cap_eff=status.get("CapEff", "").strip(),
0443:         no_new_privs=status.get("NoNewPrivs", "").strip(), ifaces=ifaces, pid=os.getpid(), uid=os.getuid())
0444: 
0445: 
0446: def _read_nsids(pid) -> dict:
0447:     return {k: os.readlink(f"/proc/{pid}/ns/{k}") for k in NS_KINDS}
0448: 
0449: 
0450: def verify_probe(probe: dict, exp: dict) -> list:
0451:     """Compare a live probe with the host observation/plan; returns the list of violations (empty = boundary holds)."""
0452:     bad = []
0453:     for k in NS_KINDS:
0454:         if probe["ns"].get(k) != exp["child_ns"].get(k):
0455:             bad.append(f"ns {k}: {probe['ns'].get(k)} != host-observed {exp['child_ns'].get(k)}")
0456:         if probe["ns"].get(k) == exp["host_ns"].get(k):
0457:             bad.append(f"ns {k}: identical to the launcher's namespace (not isolated)")
0458:     points = {m["mount_point"]: m for m in probe["mounts"]}
0459:     extra = sorted(set(points) - set(exp["mount_points"]))
0460:     if extra:
0461:         bad.append(f"unexpected mounts: {extra[:8]}")
0462:     missing = sorted(set(exp["mount_points"]) - set(points) - DEV_MOUNTS)
0463:     if missing:
0464:         bad.append(f"expected mounts absent: {missing[:8]}")
0465:     for dest in exp["ro_mounts"]:
0466:         if dest in points and "ro" not in points[dest]["options"]:
0467:             bad.append(f"{dest} is writable but planned read-only")
0468:     if probe["root"] != sorted(exp["root"]):
0469:         bad.append(f"root listing {probe['root']} != planned {sorted(exp['root'])}")
0470:     for fd, target in probe["fds"].items():
0471:         if int(fd) > 2 and target != f"/proc/{probe['pid']}/fd":     # the directory fd used for this very listing
0472:             bad.append(f"inherited fd {fd} -> {target}")
0473:         if int(fd) <= 2 and not (target.startswith(("pipe:", "socket:")) or target.startswith("/dev/")):
0474:             bad.append(f"stdio fd {fd} -> {target}")
0475:     if probe["cap_eff"].strip("0") != "":
0476:         bad.append(f"effective capabilities not empty: {probe['cap_eff']}")
0477:     if probe["no_new_privs"] != "1":
0478:         bad.append("no_new_privs not set")
0479:     if [i for i in probe["ifaces"] if i != "lo"]:
0480:         bad.append(f"network interfaces present: {probe['ifaces']}")
0481:     if probe["pid"] > 16:
0482:         bad.append(f"pid {probe['pid']} suggests the host pid namespace")
0483:     return bad
0484: 
0485: 
0486: # Sandbox-side state (set only by the bootstrap after it verified the live boundary and the pins).
0487: _ACTIVE: dict | None = None
0488: 
0489: 
0490: def activate(expectation: dict, witness: dict):
0491:     global _ACTIVE
0492:     _ACTIVE = dict(expectation=expectation, witness=witness)
0493: 
0494: 
0495: def active_witness() -> dict:
0496:     """Re-probe the live sandbox against the host observation recorded at launch. Refuses outside a verified launch."""
0497:     if _ACTIVE is None:
0498:         raise WitnessError("process was not started by the verified bwrap launcher bootstrap (no OS-level boundary witness)")
0499:     bad = verify_probe(probe_self(), _ACTIVE["expectation"])
0500:     if bad:
0501:         raise WitnessError("live OS boundary no longer matches the host observation: " + "; ".join(bad))
0502:     return dict(_ACTIVE["witness"], reverified_live=True)
0503: 
0504: 
0505: def require_real_run_release(witness: dict):
0506:     """The release gate. Closed: no release acceptance record exists, so even a verified boundary does not open real data."""
0507:     if REAL_RUN_RELEASE_ACCEPTANCE_SHA256 is None:
0508:         raise WitnessError("real-data release closed: no Director release acceptance record / trusted TRAIN-only input "
0509:                            "artifacts exist; required: " + " | ".join(RELEASE_REQUIREMENTS))
0510:     raise WitnessError("release acceptance verification is not implemented (fails closed)")

0512: 
0513: # --------------------------------------------------------------------- launcher
0514: @dataclass
0515: class LaunchResult:
0516:     status: str                      # OK | TARGET_FAILED | TARGET_SIGNALED | BOOTSTRAP_FAILED | SANDBOX_SETUP_FAILED
0517:     #                                # | HOST_VERIFICATION_FAILED | TIMEOUT
0518:     returncode: int | None
0519:     stdout: str
0520:     stderr: str
0521:     detail: str = ""
0522:     witness: dict | None = None
0523:     argv: list = field(default_factory=list)
0524: 
0525:     @property
0526:     def ok(self) -> bool:
0527:         return self.status == "OK"
0528: 
0529: 
0530: class _Pump(threading.Thread):
0531:     def __init__(self, stream, ready_marker=None):
0532:         super().__init__(daemon=True)
0533:         self.stream, self.buf, self.ready = stream, [], threading.Event()
0534:         self.marker = ready_marker
0535:         self.start()
0536: 
0537:     def run(self):
0538:         for raw in iter(self.stream.readline, b""):
0539:             line = raw.decode("utf-8", "replace")
0540:             self.buf.append(line)
0541:             if self.marker and line.startswith(self.marker):
0542:                 self.ready.set()
0543:         self.ready.set()
0544: 
0545:     def text(self):
0546:         return "".join(self.buf)
0547: 
0548: 
0549: def _marker_json(text: str, marker: str):
0550:     for line in text.splitlines():
0551:         if line.startswith(marker):
0552:             return json.loads(line[len(marker):].strip().rstrip("@"))
0553:     return None
0554: 
0555: 
0556: def launch(spec: IsolationSpec, *, host_precheck: bool = True, _tamper=None, _argv_hook=None,
0557:            _prefix=()) -> LaunchResult:
0558:     """Launch the spec's target inside the bwrap boundary and return a LaunchResult.
0559: 
0560:     Pre-launch spec violations raise OSIsolationError subclasses; everything after the process starts is reported as a
0561:     status. ``_tamper``/``_argv_hook`` exist only so tests can forge the handshake / feed a broad argv to the auditor;
0562:     ``_prefix`` lets tests run the audited bwrap argv under ``strace`` as an independent syscall-level witness."""
0563:     plan = build_plan(spec, host_precheck=host_precheck)
0564:     info_r, info_w = os.pipe()
0565:     argv = build_argv(plan, info_w)
0566:     if _argv_hook:
0567:         argv = _argv_hook(argv)
0568:     audit = audit_argv(argv, plan.protected, plan.no_expose)
0569:     nonce = secrets.token_hex(16)
0570:     proc = subprocess.Popen([*_prefix, *argv], stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env={},
0571:                             close_fds=True, pass_fds=(info_w,), start_new_session=True, cwd="/")
0572:     os.close(info_w)
0573:     out_pump, err_pump = _Pump(proc.stdout), _Pump(proc.stderr, MARK_READY)
0574: 
0575:     def finish(status, detail="", witness=None):
0576:         try:
0577:             proc.stdin.close()
0578:         except OSError:
0579:             pass
0580:         if proc.poll() is None:
0581:             try:
0582:                 os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
0583:             except OSError:
0584:                 pass
0585:         proc.wait()
0586:         out_pump.join(5), err_pump.join(5)
0587:         rc = proc.returncode
0588:         return LaunchResult(status, rc, out_pump.text(), err_pump.text(), detail, witness, argv)
0589: 
0590:     deadline = time.monotonic() + spec.timeout_s
0591:     info = _read_info(info_r, deadline)
0592:     if "child-pid" not in info:
0593:         rc = proc.wait(timeout=max(1, deadline - time.monotonic()))
0594:         out_pump.join(5), err_pump.join(5)
0595:         return LaunchResult("SANDBOX_SETUP_FAILED", rc, out_pump.text(), err_pump.text(),
0596:                             "bwrap did not report a sandbox (unsupported environment?)", None, argv)
0597:     if not err_pump.ready.wait(max(0.1, deadline - time.monotonic())):
0598:         return finish("TIMEOUT", "bootstrap never became ready")
0599:     ready = _marker_json(err_pump.text(), MARK_READY)
0600:     if ready is None:
0601:         return _classify_end(proc, out_pump, err_pump, argv, deadline, finish, nonce, None)
0602:     host_ns = _read_nsids("self")
0603:     child_ns = {k: ready["ns"][k] for k in NS_KINDS}
0604:     problems = []
0605:     for k, key in INFO_NS_KEYS.items():       # bwrap's own report of the namespaces it created
0606:         want = f"{k}:[{info.get(key)}]"
0607:         if info.get(key) is None or want != child_ns[k]:
0608:             problems.append(f"{k}: bwrap info {want} != child {child_ns[k]}")
0609:     if [k for k in NS_KINDS if child_ns[k] == host_ns[k]]:
0610:         problems.append("namespace shared with launcher: " + ",".join(k for k in NS_KINDS if child_ns[k] == host_ns[k]))
0611:     pid = info["child-pid"]
0612:     proc_view = {}
0613:     try:        # independent host-side view of the live sandbox (when /proc/<pid> is readable to us)
0614:         proc_view = dict(ns=_read_nsids(pid), mounts=parse_mountinfo(Path(f"/proc/{pid}/mountinfo").read_text()))
0615:         if proc_view["ns"] != child_ns:
0616:             problems.append("/proc/<pid>/ns differs from the child's self-report")
0617:         hm = {m["mount_point"] for m in proc_view["mounts"]}
0618:         if hm - audit["mount_points"]:
0619:             problems.append(f"host-observed unexpected mounts: {sorted(hm - audit['mount_points'])[:8]}")
0620:     except OSError:
0621:         proc_view = {}
0622:     top = sorted({d.split("/")[1] for d in audit["mount_points"] if d != "/"} | {m[2].split("/")[1] for m in plan.mounts})
0623:     exp = dict(child_ns=child_ns, host_ns=host_ns, mount_points=sorted(audit["mount_points"]), root=top,
0624:                ro_mounts=sorted(d for k, _, d in audit["binds"] if k == "ro-bind"))
0625:     if problems:
0626:         return finish("HOST_VERIFICATION_FAILED", "; ".join(problems))
0627:     witness = dict(
0628:         schema=WITNESS_SCHEMA, profile=PROFILE, nonce=nonce, launcher_pid=os.getpid(), bwrap_child_pid=pid,
0629:         argv_sha256=hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(argv).encode()).hexdigest(), host_ns=host_ns, child_ns=child_ns,
0630:         bwrap_info=info, host_proc_view_available=bool(proc_view), mount_points=exp["mount_points"],
0631:         root_listing=top, code_pins=dict(spec.code_pins),
0632:         inputs={a.alias: dict(sha256=a.sha256, approval_id=a.approval_id) for a in spec.inputs},
0633:         runtime_dirs=list(plan.runtime_dirs), runtime_lib_files=list(plan.runtime_libs), network="unshared (no interfaces)",
0634:         bootstrap_exe=f"{CODE_MOUNT}/{spec.bootstrap_root}/{BOOTSTRAP_NAME}")
0635:     hand = dict(nonce=nonce, expectation=exp, witness=witness, code_pins=spec.code_pins,
0636:                 inputs={a.alias: a.sha256 for a in spec.inputs}, target=f"{CODE_MOUNT}/{spec.target}",
0637:                 target_args=list(spec.target_args))
0638:     if _tamper:
0639:         hand = _tamper(hand)
0640:     try:
0641:         if hand is not None:        # (tests: None = the launcher sends no handshake at all)
0642:             proc.stdin.write((json.dumps(hand) + "\n").encode())
0643:             proc.stdin.flush()
0644:         proc.stdin.close()
0645:     except OSError:
0646:         pass
0647:     return _classify_end(proc, out_pump, err_pump, argv, deadline, finish, nonce, witness)
0648: 
0649: 
0650: def _read_info(fd: int, deadline: float) -> dict:
0651:     """bwrap's --info-fd JSON. Parse as soon as it is complete (a tracer such as strace may keep the pipe open, so EOF
0652:     is not required); EOF without a document means bwrap failed before creating the sandbox."""
0653:     import select
0654:     raw = b""
0655:     try:
0656:         while time.monotonic() < deadline:
0657:             if not select.select([fd], [], [], 0.2)[0]:
0658:                 continue
0659:             chunk = os.read(fd, 65536)
0660:             if not chunk:
0661:                 break
0662:             raw += chunk
0663:             try:
0664:                 return json.loads(raw.decode())
0665:             except ValueError:
0666:                 continue
0667:     finally:
0668:         os.close(fd)
0669:     return {}
0670: 
0671: 
0672: def _classify_end(proc, out_pump, err_pump, argv, deadline, finish, nonce, witness):
0673:     try:
0674:         rc = proc.wait(timeout=max(0.1, deadline - time.monotonic()))
0675:     except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
0676:         return finish("TIMEOUT", "deadline exceeded", witness)
0677:     out_pump.join(5), err_pump.join(5)
0678:     out, err = out_pump.text(), err_pump.text()
0679:     fail = _marker_json(err, MARK_FAIL)
0680:     started = any(ln.startswith(MARK_START) for ln in err.splitlines())
0681:     ended = _marker_json(err, MARK_END)
0682:     mk = lambda status, detail="": LaunchResult(status, rc, out, err, detail, witness, argv)   # noqa: E731
0683:     if fail is not None:
0684:         return mk("BOOTSTRAP_FAILED", json.dumps(fail))
0685:     if not started:
0686:         return mk("BOOTSTRAP_FAILED" if witness else "SANDBOX_SETUP_FAILED", "bootstrap did not reach the target")
0687:     if ended is None:
0688:         sig = rc - 128 if rc and rc > 128 else None
0689:         return mk("TARGET_SIGNALED", f"target ended without completing (signal {sig})" if sig else "target ended abnormally")
0690:     if ended.get("rc") != 0:
0691:         return mk("TARGET_FAILED", f"target exit code {ended.get('rc')}")
0692:     return mk("OK" if rc == 0 else "TARGET_FAILED", "" if rc == 0 else f"bwrap exit {rc}")
0693: 
0694: 
0695: def trusted_witness(result: LaunchResult) -> dict:
0696:     """Host-side acceptance of a launch as an OS-boundary witness: only a launched, host-verified, bootstrap-passed run."""
0697:     if result.witness is None or result.status not in ("OK", "TARGET_FAILED"):
0698:         raise WitnessError(f"no OS-boundary witness: launch status {result.status}")
0699:     return result.witness

===== source/os_isolation_bootstrap.py | SHA256 b11ea923feb5f539966291cf9b99487371a15dbbe270e9cd7ba5ff4cd443c3d8 =====
0001: """In-sandbox bootstrap (PID 2 inside the bwrap namespaces). Never run outside the launcher.
0002: 
0003: 1. probe the live kernel state and announce READY on stderr (the launcher then observes the sandbox from the host);
0004: 2. read ONE handshake line from stdin (stdin is then pointed at /dev/null);
0005: 3. re-verify the live boundary against the host observation, the code-tree pins and the input sha256 pins;
0006: 4. only then run the target in-process. Any failure => exit 97 with a BOOTSTRAP_FAILED marker; the target never starts.
0007: """
0008: import json
0009: import os
0010: import runpy
0011: import sys
0012: import traceback
0013: 
0014: HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
0015: sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
0016: sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
0017: import os_isolation as osi  # noqa: E402
0018: 
0019: 
0020: def fail(reason, **extra):
0021:     sys.stderr.write(f"{osi.MARK_FAIL} {json.dumps(dict(reason=reason, **extra))}@@\n")
0022:     sys.stderr.flush()
0023:     os._exit(osi.BOOTSTRAP_FAIL_RC)
0024: 
0025: 
0026: def main():
0027:     probe = osi.probe_self()
0028:     sys.stderr.write(f"{osi.MARK_READY} {json.dumps(dict(ns=probe['ns']))}@@\n")
0029:     sys.stderr.flush()
0030:     raw = sys.stdin.buffer.readline(osi.MAX_HANDSHAKE + 1)
0031:     if not raw.endswith(b"\n") or len(raw) > osi.MAX_HANDSHAKE:
0032:         fail("handshake missing or oversize")
0033:     try:
0034:         hand = json.loads(raw)
0035:         exp, witness = hand["expectation"], hand["witness"]
0036:         nonce, target = hand["nonce"], hand["target"]
0037:         if witness.get("nonce") != nonce or witness.get("schema") != osi.WITNESS_SCHEMA:
0038:             fail("witness nonce/schema mismatch")
0039:         pins, inputs, args = hand["code_pins"], hand["inputs"], hand["target_args"]
0040:     except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
0041:         fail("malformed handshake", error=str(exc))
0042:     null = os.open("/dev/null", os.O_RDONLY)
0043:     os.dup2(null, 0)
0044:     os.close(null)
0045:     bad = osi.verify_probe(osi.probe_self(), exp)
0046:     if bad:
0047:         fail("live boundary differs from host observation (forged or wrong witness)", violations=bad)
0048:     for name, pin in sorted(pins.items()):
0049:         try:
0050:             actual = osi.tree_sha256(f"{osi.CODE_MOUNT}/{name}")
0051:         except (OSError, osi.OSIsolationError) as exc:
0052:             fail("code root unreadable", root=name, error=str(exc))
0053:         if actual != pin:
0054:             fail("code tree sha256 differs from pin", root=name, expected=pin, actual=actual)
0055:     for alias, pin in sorted(inputs.items()):
0056:         try:
0057:             actual = osi.sha256_file(f"{osi.IN_MOUNT}/{alias}")
0058:         except OSError as exc:
0059:             fail("input unreadable", alias=alias, error=str(exc))
0060:         if actual != pin:
0061:             fail("input sha256 differs from pin", alias=alias, expected=pin, actual=actual)
0062:     if sorted(os.listdir(osi.IN_MOUNT) if os.path.isdir(osi.IN_MOUNT) else []) != sorted(inputs):
0063:         fail("/in content differs from the pinned inputs")
0064:     osi.activate(exp, dict(witness, bootstrap_verified_live=True, verified_code_pins=pins,
0065:                            verified_input_sha256=inputs))
0066:     sys.argv = [target, *args]
0067:     sys.stderr.write(f"{osi.MARK_START}@@\n")
0068:     sys.stderr.flush()
0069:     rc = 0
0070:     try:
0071:         runpy.run_path(target, run_name="__main__")
0072:     except SystemExit as exc:
0073:         rc = exc.code if isinstance(exc.code, int) else (0 if exc.code is None else 1)
0074:     except BaseException:
0075:         traceback.print_exc()
0076:         rc = 1
0077:     sys.stdout.flush()
0078:     sys.stderr.write(f"{osi.MARK_END} {json.dumps(dict(rc=rc))}@@\n")
0079:     sys.stderr.flush()
0080:     os._exit(rc & 0xFF)
0081: 
0082: 
0083: if __name__ == "__main__":
0084:     main()

0270: def mutate(path, value):
0271:     def apply(auth):
0272:         a = copy.deepcopy(auth)
0273:         node = a
0274:         for p in path[:-1]:
0275:             node = node[p]
0276:         if value is KeyError:
0277:             del node[path[-1]]
0278:         else:
0279:             node[path[-1]] = value
0280:         return a
0281:     return apply

0415: def test_curated_frames_feeds_and_engine_equal_original_source_baseline(tmp_path):
0416:     w = mk(tmp_path)
0417:     run, ledger, ctx, root = curated_prepare(w)
0418:     frames, feeds, audit = run()
0419:     ref_frames, ref_feeds = reference_prepare(w)
0420:     assert set(frames) == set(ref_frames) == {"AAA", "BBB"}
0421:     for s in frames:
0422:         pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(frames[s], ref_frames[s])
0423:         assert len(frames[s]) == audit["stock"][s]["target_rows"] + 60
0424:     for n in feeds:
0425:         pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(feeds[n], ref_feeds[n])
0426:     assert audit["clock_events"] == sum(a["clock_rows"] for a in audit["stock"].values())
0427:     assert audit["terminal_bar_policy"] == ca.TERMINAL_BAR_POLICY and all(not a["terminal_sentinel_row_present"] for a in audit["stock"].values())
0428:     for row in ledger.files:       # whole artifact consumed to EOF == pinned sha; nothing else opened
0429:         assert row["consumed_prefix_sha256"] == sha(Path(row["path"]).read_bytes()) and row["physical_bytes_read"] == row["size_bytes"]
0430:         assert row["reached_eof"] is True
0431:     assert sorted(ca.ARTIFACT_OPENS[-4:]) == sorted(ctx.paths)
0432:     # engine on the ORIGINAL-source reference frames == engine on the curated frames (same process, deterministic)
0433:     out_ref, out_cur = tmp_path / "out_ref", tmp_path / "out_cur"
0434:     res_ref = run_engine(lambda r, p, b: (*reference_prepare(w), dict(slice_sha256="REFERENCE")), out_ref, w)
0435:     res_cur = run_engine(lambda r, p, b: run(), out_cur, w)
0436:     assert file_hashes(out_ref) == file_hashes(out_cur)
0437:     assert res_ref["metrics"] == res_cur["metrics"] and res_cur["metrics"] is not None

0451: 
0452: def amut(name, key, value):
0453:     """Mutate one artifact entry of the authorization by artifact NAME (value may be a callable of the entry)."""
0454:     def apply(auth):
0455:         a = copy.deepcopy(auth)
0456:         entry = next(e for e in a["artifacts"] if e["name"] == name)
0457:         entry[key] = value(entry) if callable(value) else value
0458:         return a
0459:     return apply
0460: 
0461: 
0462: def stock_swap(blob):
0463:     lines = blob.split(b"\n")
0464:     lines[10], lines[11] = lines[11], lines[10]
0465:     return b"\n".join(lines)
0466: 
0467: 
0468: def only(role, name, fn):
0469:     return lambda r, n, b: fn(b) if (r, n) == (role, name) else b
0470: 
0471: 
0472: def grid_first_row_before_train(blob):
0473:     head, *rest = blob.split(b"\n", 1)
0474:     return head + b"\n2023-08-31T09:15:00+05:30,100.0\n" + rest[0]

0554: def test_source_root_audit_in_process(tmp_path):
0555:     allowed = [sys.prefix, sys.base_prefix, str(PLATFORM), "/usr/lib"]
0556:     ca.audit_source_roots(allowed)
0557:     import tempfile
0558:     evil = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="evil_", dir="/tmp"))      # outside every allowed root regardless of pytest basetemp
0559:     (evil / "evilmod_x.py").write_text("X=1\n")
0560:     sys.path.insert(0, str(evil))
0561:     try:
0562:         with pytest.raises(ca.SourceRootEscape):
0563:             ca.audit_source_roots(allowed)
0564:         importlib.import_module("evilmod_x")
0565:         sys.path.remove(str(evil))
0566:         with pytest.raises(ca.SourceRootEscape, match="evilmod_x"):
0567:             ca.audit_source_roots(allowed)
0568:     finally:
0569:         sys.modules.pop("evilmod_x", None)
0570:         if str(evil) in sys.path:
0571:             sys.path.remove(str(evil))
0572:         shutil.rmtree(evil, ignore_errors=True)

0575: # =================================================================== 4. host refusals open no data and never launch
0576: def refusal_worlds(tmp_path):
0577:     yield "wrong_release_pin", mk(tmp_path, "a", pin_override="0" * 64), rr.ReleaseError, "external pin"
0578:     yield "malformed_release_pin", mk(tmp_path, "b", pin_override="not-a-pin"), rr.ReleaseError, "pin"
0579:     yield "unknown_issuer", mk(tmp_path, "c", trusted={"SOMEONE_ELSE": ["SYNTHETIC_ONLY"]}), rr.ReleaseError, "not externally trusted"
0580:     yield "no_trusted_issuers", mk(tmp_path, "d", trusted={}), rr.ReleaseError, "not externally trusted"
0581:     yield "real_classification_closed", mk(tmp_path, "e", classification=h.CLASSIFICATION, data_origin="REAL_CURATED",
0582:                                            trusted={iwm.ISSUER: ["REAL_TRAIN_DIAGNOSTIC", "SYNTHETIC_ONLY"]}), rr.ReleaseError, "real-data release closed"
0583:     yield "synthetic_scope_for_real_classification", mk(
0584:         tmp_path, "f", classification=h.CLASSIFICATION, data_origin="REAL_CURATED", scope="SYNTHETIC_ONLY"), rr.ReleaseError, "does not match"
0585:     yield "real_scope_for_synthetic_classification", mk(tmp_path, "g", scope="REAL_TRAIN_DIAGNOSTIC"), rr.ReleaseError, "does not match"
0586:     yield "unknown_classification", mk(tmp_path, "h", classification="MARKET_REPLAY", data_origin="REAL_CURATED"), h.AuthorizationError, "not recognised"
0587:     yield "record_wrong_exec", mk(tmp_path, "i", record_mutator=lambda r: dict(r, execution_id="OTHER")), rr.ReleaseError, "execution_id"
0588:     yield "record_wrong_auth_sha", mk(tmp_path, "j", record_mutator=lambda r: dict(r, authorization_sha256="1" * 64)), rr.ReleaseError, "authorization_sha256"
0589:     yield "record_wrong_profile", mk(tmp_path, "k", record_mutator=lambda r: dict(r, isolation_profile="OTHER")), rr.ReleaseError, "isolation_profile"
0590:     yield "record_wrong_code_pin", mk(tmp_path, "l", record_mutator=lambda r: dict(r, code_pins=dict(r["code_pins"], harness="1" * 64))), rr.ReleaseError, "code_pins"
0591:     yield "record_wrong_input_pin", mk(tmp_path, "m", record_mutator=lambda r: dict(r, input_pins=dict(r["input_pins"], **{"stock_AAA.csv": "1" * 64}))), rr.ReleaseError, "input_pins"
0592:     yield "auth_unknown_schema", mk(tmp_path, "n", auth_mutator=lambda a: dict(a, schema="WP004_CURATED_ARTIFACT_AUTHORIZATION_V9")), h.AuthorizationError, "schema"
0593:     yield "auth_extra_flag", mk(tmp_path, "o", auth_mutator=lambda a: dict(a, authorizes_real_data_read=True)), h.AuthorizationError, "keys"
0594:     yield "auth_symbol_missing", mk(tmp_path, "p", auth_mutator=lambda a: dict(a, artifacts=[e for e in a["artifacts"] if e["name"] != "BBB"])), h.AuthorizationError, "bijection"
