0597: def test_host_refusals_open_no_artifact_and_never_launch(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
0598:     seen = []
0599:     for label, w, exc, match in refusal_worlds(tmp_path):
0600:         with monkeypatch.context() as mp:
0601:             spy = Spy(mp, [w.art_dir])
0602:             before = list(ca.ARTIFACT_OPENS)
0603:             with pytest.raises(exc, match=match):
0604:                 cl.run_curated(w.cfg())
0605:             assert spy.touched == [] and spy.launched == [] and ca.ARTIFACT_OPENS == before, label
0606:         seen.append(label)
0607:     assert len(seen) == 16

0622:     else:
0623:         cfg["extra"] = 1

0782: # =================================================================== 6. negative controls through real bwrap
0783: def refused(run):
0784:     r = run.result
0785:     assert r.status == "TARGET_FAILED" and r.returncode == 2, (r.status, r.stderr[-1500:])
0786:     line = [ln for ln in r.stderr.splitlines() if ln.startswith(ct.REFUSED)][0]
0787:     return json.loads(line[len(ct.REFUSED):].strip().rstrip("@"))

0790: @pytest.mark.parametrize("case,error", [
0791:     ("stock_poison_at_train_end", "ChronologyError"), ("stock_count_mismatch", "CuratedArtifactError"),
0792:     ("stock_schema_header", "ShapeError"), ("grid_poison_after_cutoff", "ChronologyError"),
0793:     ("stock_hash_mismatch", None)])
0794: def test_sandbox_artifact_violations_fail_closed(tmp_path, case, error):
0795:     kw, exc, match = LOADER_CASES[case]
0796:     w = mk(tmp_path, **kw)
0797:     if error is None:     # auth sha differs from bytes: host pre-check refuses; sandbox re-hash refuses when bypassed
0798:         with pytest.raises(osi.PinMismatch):
0799:             cl.run_curated(w.cfg())
0800:         return
0801:     run = cl.run_curated(w.cfg())
0802:     marker = refused(run)
0803:     assert marker["error"] == error and match.split()[0].lower() in marker["message"].lower() or error in marker["error"]
0804:     assert marker["artifact_opens"]                      # it reached the loader legitimately (approved bytes) ...
0805:     assert not (w.out_dir / "run" / ct.RECEIPT_NAME).exists() and not (w.out_dir / "run" / "native_report.json").exists()   # ... and no engine output

0079: - A mutant that cannot be applied counts as a survivor in the driver. None occurred.
0080: - `work/wp004_train_harness/test_tmp/` holds many pytest scratch directories. They are not part of the deliverable.
0081: 
0082: ## Manifest
0083: `SHA256SUMS.txt` in this directory covers the receipt, logs, evidence, fingerprints, interruption record and the harness and test sources.

0097: # --------------------------------------------------------------------- hashing
0098: def is_sha256(value) -> bool:
0099:     return isinstance(value, str) and len(value) == 64 and set(value) <= HEX64
0100: 
0101: 
0102: def _positive_int(value, label):
0103:     """Integral, positive, never bool."""
0104:     if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value < 1:
0105:         raise HarnessError(f"{label} must be a positive integer (not bool), got {value!r}")
0106:     return value
0107: 
0108: 
0109: INTERVAL_KINDS = ("HEADER", "TRAIN_DATA", "PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT")
0110: 
0111: 
0112: def normalize_intervals(intervals):
0113:     """Sorted, non-overlapping, integral half-open [start, end) byte intervals tagged with a kind."""
0114:     out, last_end = [], 0
0115:     for item in intervals:
0116:         try:
0117:             start, end, kind = item
0118:         except (TypeError, ValueError):
0119:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"malformed interval {item!r}") from None
0120:         for value, label in ((start, "start"), (end, "end")):
0121:             if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int):
0122:                 raise ReadBoundaryError(f"interval {label} must be an integer, got {value!r}")
0123:         if kind not in INTERVAL_KINDS:
0124:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"unknown interval kind {kind!r}")
0125:         if start < 0 or end <= start or start < last_end:
0126:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"intervals must be positive-length, sorted, non-overlapping: {item!r}")
0127:         out.append((start, end, kind))
0128:         last_end = end
0129:     if not out:
0130:         raise ReadBoundaryError("empty interval list")
0131:     return tuple(out)
0132: 
0133: 
0134: def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
0135:     return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
0136: 
0137: 
0138: def sha256_code_file(path: Path) -> str:
0139:     """Whole-file hash for CODE/METADATA files only (never market data)."""
0140:     digest = hashlib.sha256()
0141:     with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
0142:         for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1 << 20), b""):
0143:             digest.update(chunk)
0144:     return digest.hexdigest()
0145: 
0146: 
0147: def tree_digest(files: dict) -> str:
0148:     return sha256_bytes("".join(f"{k}\t{files[k]}\n" for k in sorted(files)).encode())

0152: def verify_snapshot(snapshot_dir, manifest_path=None, *, expected_manifest_sha256=None) -> dict:
0153:     """Re-hash every snapshot file against SNAPSHOT_MANIFEST.json; fail closed."""
0154:     snapshot = Path(snapshot_dir).resolve()
0155:     manifest_path = Path(manifest_path) if manifest_path else snapshot.parent / "SNAPSHOT_MANIFEST.json"
0156:     manifest_sha = sha256_code_file(manifest_path)
0157:     if expected_manifest_sha256 is not None:
0158:         if not is_sha256(expected_manifest_sha256):
0159:             raise CodeDriftError("malformed expected manifest hash")
0160:         if manifest_sha != expected_manifest_sha256:
0161:             raise CodeDriftError("snapshot manifest hash differs from pin")
0162:     manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
0163:     if manifest.get("head") != EXPECTED_HEAD:
0164:         raise CodeDriftError(f"snapshot HEAD {manifest.get('head')} != {EXPECTED_HEAD}")
0165:     files = manifest.get("files")
0166:     if not isinstance(files, dict) or not files:
0167:         raise CodeDriftError("manifest has no files")
0168:     bad_format = [k for k, v in files.items() if not is_sha256(v)]
0169:     if bad_format:
0170:         raise CodeDriftError(f"malformed manifest hashes: {sorted(bad_format)[:5]}")
0171:     if files.get(PROTECTED_INDICATOR[0]) != PROTECTED_INDICATOR[1]:
0172:         raise CodeDriftError("protected indicator hash missing from manifest")
0173:     mismatched, missing = [], []
0174:     for rel, expected in files.items():
0175:         path = snapshot / rel
0176:         if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
0177:             missing.append(rel)
0178:         elif sha256_code_file(path) != expected:
0179:             mismatched.append(rel)
0180:     extra = []
0181:     for base, dirs, names in os.walk(snapshot):
0182:         for name in names + dirs:
0183:             full = Path(base) / name
0184:             rel = full.relative_to(snapshot).as_posix()
0185:             if full.is_symlink():
0186:                 extra.append(rel)
0187:             elif full.is_file() and rel not in files:
0188:                 extra.append(rel)
0189:     if mismatched or missing or extra:
0190:         raise CodeDriftError(
0191:             f"snapshot drift: mismatched={sorted(mismatched)[:5]} missing={sorted(missing)[:5]} extra={sorted(extra)[:5]}")
0192:     return dict(head=manifest["head"], manifest_sha256=manifest_sha, file_count=len(files),
0193:                 tree_sha256=tree_digest(files), protected_indicator=dict(zip(("path", "sha256"), PROTECTED_INDICATOR)),
0194:                 files=dict(files))

0260: class BoundedLineReader:
0261:     """Binary line reader over an unbuffered file: counts every physical byte and hashes consumed lines."""
0262: 
0263:     def __init__(self, path, *, chunk_size=DEFAULT_CHUNK, max_physical_bytes=None, intervals=None,
0264:                  expected_commitment=None):
0265:         # Every argument is validated BEFORE the file is opened or stat'ed.
0266:         self.chunk_size = _positive_int(chunk_size, "chunk_size")
0267:         self.max_physical_bytes = None if max_physical_bytes is None else _positive_int(max_physical_bytes, "max_physical_bytes")
0268:         self.intervals = None if intervals is None else normalize_intervals(intervals)
0269:         if expected_commitment is not None and not is_sha256(expected_commitment):
0270:             raise HarnessError("malformed expected content commitment")
0271:         self.expected_commitment = expected_commitment
0272:         self.path = Path(path)
0273:         self.size_bytes = self.path.stat().st_size
0274:         if self.intervals is not None and self.intervals[-1][1] > self.size_bytes:
0275:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: authorized interval ends beyond file size")
0276:         self.physical_bytes_read = 0
0277:         self.read_calls = 0
0278:         self.consumed_bytes = 0
0279:         self.rows = 0
0280:         self.reached_eof = False
0281:         self.line_kind = None          # kind of the authorized interval the most recently consumed line came from
0282:         self.first_data_offset = None
0283:         self.read_ranges = []          # (offset, length) of every physical read, in order
0284:         self._buffer = b""
0285:         self._digest = hashlib.sha256()
0286:         self._ivl, self._pos, self._kind, self._read_kind = -1, 0, None, None
0287:         _IN_HARNESS_READ.active = True
0288:         try:
0289:             self._handle = builtins.open(self.path, "rb", buffering=0)
0290:         finally:
0291:             _IN_HARNESS_READ.active = False
0292: 
0293:     def _next_read_size(self) -> int:
0294:         """Bytes the next physical read may request: min(chunk, interval remaining, cap remaining); fails closed at 0."""
0295:         cap_left = None if self.max_physical_bytes is None else self.max_physical_bytes - self.physical_bytes_read
0296:         plan_left = None
0297:         if self.intervals is not None:
0298:             count = len(self.intervals)
0299:             while self._ivl < 0 or (self._ivl < count and self._pos >= self.intervals[self._ivl][1]):
0300:                 nxt = self._ivl + 1
0301:                 if nxt >= count:
0302:                     self._ivl = count
0303:                     break
0304:                 if self._buffer:
0305:                     raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: line straddles an authorized interval boundary")
0306:                 start, _end, kind = self.intervals[nxt]
0307:                 if self._pos != start:
0308:                     self._handle.seek(start)       # repositioning only; no bytes are read
0309:                     self._pos = start
0310:                 self._ivl, self._kind = nxt, kind
0311:             plan_left = 0 if self._ivl >= count else self.intervals[self._ivl][1] - self._pos
0312:         limit = self.chunk_size
0313:         for bound in (plan_left, cap_left):
0314:             if bound is not None:
0315:                 limit = min(limit, bound)
0316:         if limit <= 0:
0317:             if plan_left is not None and plan_left <= 0:
0318:                 raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: authorized read plan exhausted")
0319:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: physical read cap {self.max_physical_bytes} reached")
0320:         return limit
0321: 
0322:     def next_line(self):
0323:         while True:
0324:             index = self._buffer.find(b"\n")
0325:             if index >= 0:
0326:                 line, self._buffer = self._buffer[:index + 1], self._buffer[index + 1:]
0327:                 return self._consume(line)
0328:             if len(self._buffer) > MAX_LINE_BYTES:
0329:                 raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: line exceeds {MAX_LINE_BYTES} bytes")
0330:             if self.reached_eof:
0331:                 if self._buffer:
0332:                     line, self._buffer = self._buffer, b""
0333:                     return self._consume(line)
0334:                 return None
0335:             limit = self._next_read_size()       # raises BEFORE the first prohibited byte is requested
0336:             chunk = self._handle.read(limit)
0337:             self.read_calls += 1
0338:             if len(chunk) > limit:
0339:                 raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: read returned {len(chunk)} > requested {limit}")
0340:             if not chunk:
0341:                 if self.intervals is not None:
0342:                     raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: file ended inside an authorized interval")
0343:                 self.reached_eof = True
0344:             else:
0345:                 self.read_ranges.append((self._pos, len(chunk)))
0346:                 self._pos += len(chunk)
0347:                 self._read_kind = self._kind
0348:             self.physical_bytes_read += len(chunk)
0349:             if self.max_physical_bytes is not None and self.physical_bytes_read > self.max_physical_bytes:
0350:                 raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: physical read cap {self.max_physical_bytes} exceeded")
0351:             self._buffer += chunk
0352: 
0353:     def _consume(self, line: bytes) -> bytes:
0354:         if len(line) > MAX_LINE_BYTES:
0355:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: line exceeds {MAX_LINE_BYTES} bytes")
0356:         if self.first_data_offset is None and self._read_kind != "HEADER":
0357:             # lines never straddle intervals, so the first non-header line starts its interval
0358:             self.first_data_offset = self.intervals[self._ivl][0] if self.intervals is not None else 0
0359:         self.line_kind = self._read_kind
0360:         self._digest.update(line)
0361:         self.consumed_bytes += len(line)
0362:         self.rows += 1
0363:         return line
0364: 
0365:     @property
0366:     def data_contiguous_from_start(self) -> bool:
0367:         """True when the first non-header row sits directly after the header (nothing skipped from file start)."""
0368:         if self.intervals is None:
0369:             return True
0370:         header_end = self.intervals[0][1] if self.intervals[0][2] == "HEADER" else 0
0371:         return self.first_data_offset is not None and self.first_data_offset == header_end
0372: 
0373:     def close(self):
0374:         self._handle.close()
0375: 
0376:     def record(self, *, strict_prefix=True) -> dict:
0377:         overread = self.physical_bytes_read - self.consumed_bytes
0378:         if overread < 0 or overread >= self.chunk_size:
0379:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: overread {overread} outside [0,{self.chunk_size})")
0380:         if self.max_physical_bytes is not None and self.physical_bytes_read > self.max_physical_bytes:
0381:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: physical read cap exceeded")
0382:         if strict_prefix and (self.reached_eof or self.physical_bytes_read >= self.size_bytes):
0383:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{self.path.name}: bounded access not proven (read reached end of file)")
0384:         digest = self._digest.hexdigest()
0385:         planned = None
0386:         if self.intervals is not None:
0387:             planned = sum(end - start for start, end, _ in self.intervals)
0388:             if self.physical_bytes_read != planned or self.consumed_bytes != planned:
0389:                 raise ReadBoundaryError(
0390:                     f"{self.path.name}: authorized plan not exactly consumed (planned={planned} "
0391:                     f"physical={self.physical_bytes_read} consumed={self.consumed_bytes})")
0392:         if self.expected_commitment is not None and digest != self.expected_commitment:
0393:             raise ContentCommitmentError(f"{self.path.name}: consumed bytes do not match supplied content commitment")
0394:         return dict(path=str(self.path), size_bytes=self.size_bytes, chunk_size=self.chunk_size,
0395:                     physical_bytes_read=self.physical_bytes_read, consumed_bytes=self.consumed_bytes,
0396:                     overread_bytes=overread, read_calls=self.read_calls, lines_consumed=self.rows,
0397:                     reached_eof=self.reached_eof, consumed_prefix_sha256=digest,
0398:                     max_physical_bytes=self.max_physical_bytes, planned_bytes=planned,
0399:                     read_ranges=[list(r) for r in self.read_ranges],
0400:                     content_commitment_verified=self.expected_commitment is not None)
0401: 
0402: 
0403: def _csv_lines(reader: BoundedLineReader, name: str):
0404:     """Decoded, shape-checked text lines for csv; stops exactly where the consumer stops."""
0405:     first = True
0406:     while True:
0407:         raw = reader.next_line()
0408:         if raw is None:
0409:             return
0410:         body = raw[3:] if first and raw.startswith(b"\xef\xbb\xbf") else raw
0411:         first = False
0412:         text = body.decode("utf-8")
0413:         text = text[:-2] if text.endswith("\r\n") else text[:-1] if text.endswith("\n") else text
0414:         if "\r" in text or '"' in text:
0415:             raise ShapeError(f"{name}: unsupported quoting/line terminator in row {reader.rows}")
0416:         yield text
0417: 
0418: 
0419: def _dict_reader(reader, name, required):
0420:     dict_reader = csv.DictReader(_csv_lines(reader, name))
0421:     fields = dict_reader.fieldnames or []
0422:     if len(set(fields)) != len(fields):
0423:         raise ShapeError(f"{name}: duplicate header columns")
0424:     missing = [c for c in required if c not in fields]
0425:     if missing:
0426:         raise ShapeError(f"{name}: missing columns {missing}")
0427:     return dict_reader
0428: 
0429: 
0430: def _checked(row, name):
0431:     if None in row or any(v is None for v in row.values()):
0432:         raise ShapeError(f"{name}: row shape differs from header")
0433:     return row

0447: def _reader_for(path, file_plan, chunk_size, max_physical_bytes):
0448:     """Reader bound to the per-file authorization when one exists (plan lookup precedes any open)."""
0449:     if file_plan is None:
0450:         return BoundedLineReader(path, chunk_size=chunk_size, max_physical_bytes=max_physical_bytes)
0451:     if str(Path(path)) != file_plan.path:
0452:         raise UnplannedFileError(f"{path}: differs from authorized canonical path {file_plan.path}")
0453:     return BoundedLineReader(path, chunk_size=chunk_size, max_physical_bytes=file_plan.max_physical_bytes,
0454:                              intervals=file_plan.intervals, expected_commitment=file_plan.content_commitment_sha256)
0455: 
0456: 
0457: def load_stock_block_bounded(path, sessions, warmup_bars, *, train_end_exclusive, record=None,
0458:                              chunk_size=DEFAULT_CHUNK, max_physical_bytes=None, strict_prefix=True,
0459:                              file_plan=None, train_start=None, pretrain_policy="REJECT"):
0460:     """Chronological prefix of one stock file: warmup + target sessions + exactly one sentinel."""
0461:     import pandas as pd
0462:     w = _worker()
0463:     path = Path(path)
0464:     target_dates = [pd.Timestamp(x).date() for x in sessions]
0465:     target_set = set(target_dates)
0466:     first_date, last_date = target_dates[0], target_dates[-1]
0467:     end_date = pd.Timestamp(train_end_exclusive).date()
0468:     if last_date >= end_date:
0469:         raise ChronologyError(f"{path.name}: block extends to/after TRAIN end {end_date}")
0470:     from collections import deque
0471:     warmup = deque(maxlen=warmup_bars)
0472:     target, sentinel, seen = [], None, set()
0473:     pretrain_start = pd.Timestamp(train_start).date() if train_start is not None else None
0474:     pretrain_rows = 0
0475:     reader = _reader_for(path, file_plan, chunk_size, max_physical_bytes)
0476:     try:
0477:         rows = _dict_reader(reader, path.name, ["open", "high", "low", "close", "volume"])
0478:         fieldnames = rows.fieldnames
0479:         ts_column = "timestamp" if "timestamp" in fieldnames else "date" if "date" in fieldnames else None
0480:         if ts_column is None:
0481:             raise ShapeError(f"{path}: no timestamp/date column")
0482:         started, previous, first_raw = False, None, None
0483:         for row in rows:
0484:             _checked(row, path.name)
0485:             ts = w.exchange_ts(row[ts_column])
0486:             if first_raw is None:
0487:                 first_raw = row[ts_column]
0488:                 # only meaningful when nothing was skipped between the header and the first data row
0489:                 if record is not None and reader.data_contiguous_from_start and first_raw != record.first_timestamp:
0490:                     raise ManifestMismatchError(f"{path.name}: first row {first_raw!r} != manifest {record.first_timestamp!r}")
0491:             if previous is not None and ts <= previous:
0492:                 raise ChronologyError(f"{path.name}: timestamps not strictly increasing at {ts}")
0493:             previous = ts
0494:             day = ts.date()
0495:             if day >= end_date:
0496:                 raise ChronologyError(f"{path.name}: row {day} at/after TRAIN end {end_date}")
0497:             if pretrain_start is not None:
0498:                 kind = reader.line_kind
0499:                 if day < pretrain_start:
0500:                     if pretrain_policy != "WARMUP_PROVENANCE_NO_FIT":
0501:                         raise PreTrainSelectionError(f"{path.name}: preTRAIN row {day} selected but not authorized")
0502:                     if reader.intervals is not None and kind != "PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT":
0503:                         raise PreTrainSelectionError(f"{path.name}: preTRAIN row {day} outside a PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT interval")
0504:                     if day >= first_date:
0505:                         raise PreTrainSelectionError(f"{path.name}: preTRAIN row {day} is not warmup")
0506:                     pretrain_rows += 1
0507:                 elif kind == "PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT":
0508:                     raise PreTrainSelectionError(f"{path.name}: TRAIN-dated row {day} inside a PRETRAIN interval")
0509:             if not started:
0510:                 if day < first_date:
0511:                     warmup.append(row)
0512:                     continue
0513:                 if day > first_date:
0514:                     raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: first target session missing {first_date}")
0515:                 started = True
0516:             if day <= last_date:
0517:                 if day not in target_set:
0518:                     raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: unexpected real session {day} inside declared block")
0519:                 target.append(row)
0520:                 seen.add(day)
0521:                 continue
0522:             sentinel = row
0523:             break
0524:         ledger_row = reader.record(strict_prefix=strict_prefix) if sentinel is not None else None
0525:     finally:
0526:         reader.close()
0527:     if len(warmup) != warmup_bars:
0528:         raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: expected {warmup_bars} warmup bars, got {len(warmup)}")
0529:     if seen != target_set:
0530:         raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: missing target sessions {sorted(target_set - seen)}")
0531:     if not target:
0532:         raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: zero target rows")
0533:     if sentinel is None:
0534:         raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: boundary sentinel missing")
0535:     if record is not None:
0536:         last_allowed = w.exchange_ts(record.last_timestamp)
0537:         if w.exchange_ts(sentinel[ts_column]) > last_allowed or reader.rows > record.row_count:
0538:             raise ManifestMismatchError(f"{path.name}: consumed rows exceed manifest extent")
0539:     warmup_pretrain = (sum(1 for r in warmup if w.exchange_ts(r[ts_column]).date() < pretrain_start)
0540:                        if pretrain_start is not None else None)
0541:     frame = w.convert_stock_rows(list(warmup) + target + [sentinel])
0542:     warm_frame, target_frame, sentinel_frame = frame.iloc[:warmup_bars], frame.iloc[warmup_bars:-1], frame.iloc[-1:]
0543:     counts = {}
0544:     for ts in target_frame["timestamp"]:
0545:         key = str(w.exchange_ts(ts).date())
0546:         counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
0547:     if w.exchange_ts(sentinel_frame["timestamp"].iloc[0]) <= w.exchange_ts(target_frame["timestamp"].iloc[-1]):
0548:         raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: sentinel is not after target")
0549:     audit = {
0550:         "warmup_rows": len(warm_frame), "warmup_first_utc": w.utc_iso(warm_frame["timestamp"].iloc[0]),
0551:         "warmup_last_utc": w.utc_iso(warm_frame["timestamp"].iloc[-1]), "target_rows": len(target_frame),
0552:         "target_first_utc": w.utc_iso(target_frame["timestamp"].iloc[0]),
0553:         "target_last_utc": w.utc_iso(target_frame["timestamp"].iloc[-1]), "target_session_counts": counts,
0554:         "sentinel_rows": 1, "sentinel_utc": w.utc_iso(sentinel_frame["timestamp"].iloc[0]),
0555:         "consumed_prefix_sha256": ledger_row["consumed_prefix_sha256"], "consumed_bytes": ledger_row["consumed_bytes"],
0556:         "physical_bytes_read": ledger_row["physical_bytes_read"],
0557:         "pretrain_policy": pretrain_policy, "pretrain_rows_consumed": pretrain_rows,
0558:         "warmup_rows_dated_before_train_start": warmup_pretrain,
0559:         "sentinel_row_authorized": None if file_plan is None else file_plan.terminal_boundary_row_authorized,
0560:         "train_scope": TRAIN_SCOPE_DISCLOSURE,
0561:     }
0562:     return frame, audit, ledger_row
0563: 
0564: 
0565: def load_grid_prefix_bounded(records, protocol, cutoff_utc, *, allowed_grid_roots, chunk_size=DEFAULT_CHUNK,
0566:                              max_physical_bytes=None, strict_prefix=True, read_plan=None, train_start=None):
0567:     """Causal Nifty/VIX prefix. Declared sha256 values are compared as METADATA only (never re-hashed)."""
0568:     import pandas as pd
0569:     expected = protocol["grid_dataset"]["feeds"]
0570:     delay = int(protocol["grid_dataset"]["availability_delay_minutes"])
0571:     _, end_date = _train_bounds(protocol)
0572:     grid_train_start = pd.Timestamp(train_start).date() if train_start is not None else None
0573:     feeds, audit, ledger_rows = {}, {}, []
0574:     for name in sorted(expected):
0575:         record, sealed = records[name], expected[name]
0576:         if not is_sha256(sealed.get("sha256")) or not is_sha256(record.get("sha256")):
0577:             raise ManifestMismatchError(f"{name}: malformed grid sha256")
0578:         if record["sha256"] != sealed["sha256"]:
0579:             raise ValueError(f"{name}: local manifest SHA mismatch")
0580:         path = _safe_file(Path(record["path"]), allowed_grid_roots, f"grid feed {name}")
0581:         ts_column = sealed["timestamp_column"]
0582:         file_plan = read_plan.require(path) if read_plan is not None else None
0583:         reader = _reader_for(path, file_plan, chunk_size, max_physical_bytes)
0584:         rows, previous, boundary_rows = [], None, 0
0585:         try:
0586:             dict_reader = _dict_reader(reader, name, [ts_column, "close"])
0587:             for row in dict_reader:
0588:                 _checked(row, name)
0589:                 source = pd.to_datetime(row[ts_column], utc=True)
0590:                 if source.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata").date() >= end_date:
0591:                     raise ChronologyError(f"{name}: grid row {source} at/after TRAIN end {end_date}")
0592:                 if grid_train_start is not None and (source.tz_convert("Asia/Kolkata").date() < grid_train_start
0593:                                                      or reader.line_kind == "PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT"):
0594:                     raise PreTrainSelectionError(f"{name}: preTRAIN grid row {source} would enter a retained feed")
0595:                 availability = source + pd.Timedelta(minutes=delay)
0596:                 if previous is not None and availability <= previous:
0597:                     raise ChronologyError(f"{name}: grid timestamps not strictly increasing at {availability}")
0598:                 previous = availability
0599:                 if availability > cutoff_utc:
0600:                     boundary_rows = 1  # one row is read to detect the cutoff; it is discarded, never retained
0601:                     break
0602:                 rows.append({"timestamp": availability, "close": row["close"]})
0603:             ledger_row = reader.record(strict_prefix=strict_prefix)
0604:         finally:
0605:             reader.close()
0606:         if not rows:
0607:             raise ValueError(f"{name}: no causal grid prefix")
0608:         if not boundary_rows:
0609:             raise ReadBoundaryError(f"{name}: grid cutoff boundary row not observed")
0610:         frame = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0611:         frame["timestamp"] = pd.to_datetime(frame["timestamp"], utc=True)
0612:         frame["close"] = pd.to_numeric(frame["close"], errors="raise")
0613:         if (frame["timestamp"] > cutoff_utc).any():
0614:             raise ValueError(f"{name}: post-cutoff row retained")
0615:         feeds[name] = frame
0616:         audit[name] = {
0617:             "declared_sha256_unverified_metadata": sealed["sha256"],
0618:             "consumed_prefix_sha256": ledger_row["consumed_prefix_sha256"], "consumed_bytes": ledger_row["consumed_bytes"],
0619:             "physical_bytes_read": ledger_row["physical_bytes_read"], "rows": len(frame),
0620:             "first_available_utc": frame["timestamp"].iloc[0].isoformat(),
0621:             "last_available_utc": frame["timestamp"].iloc[-1].isoformat(), "cutoff_utc": cutoff_utc.isoformat(),
0622:             "rows_after_cutoff_retained": 0, "boundary_rows_parsed_and_discarded": boundary_rows,
0623:             "boundary_row_authorized": None if file_plan is None else file_plan.terminal_boundary_row_authorized,
0624:             "boundary_row_affects_retained_feed": False, "train_scope": TRAIN_SCOPE_DISCLOSURE,
0625:         }
0626:         ledger_rows.append(ledger_row)
0627:     return feeds, audit, ledger_rows

0629: 
0630: # ------------------------------------------------------------ roots / manifest
0631: def _inside(path: Path, roots) -> bool:
0632:     return any(path == r or path.is_relative_to(r) for r in roots)
0633: 
0634: 
0635: def _resolve_roots(roots, label):
0636:     out = []
0637:     for r in roots:
0638:         r = Path(r)
0639:         if not r.is_absolute() or ".." in r.parts:
0640:             raise UnsafeRootError(f"{label} root must be absolute without '..': {r}")
0641:         out.append(r.resolve())
0642:     if not out:
0643:         raise UnsafeRootError(f"no {label} roots declared")
0644:     return tuple(out)
0645: 
0646: 
0647: def _safe_file(path: Path, roots, label) -> Path:
0648:     if not path.is_absolute() or ".." in path.parts:
0649:         raise UnsafeRootError(f"{label}: unsafe path {path}")
0650:     resolved = path.resolve()
0651:     if not _inside(resolved, roots):
0652:         raise UnsafeRootError(f"{label}: {resolved} outside declared roots")
0653:     if resolved != Path(os.path.abspath(path)) or not resolved.is_file():
0654:         raise UnsafeRootError(f"{label}: {path} is not a plain file within roots (symlink/missing)")
0655:     return resolved

0658: # ------------------------------------------------- real-execution authorization
0659: CONTRACT_ID = "WP-R5-004/decision_support/DIRECTOR_DIAGNOSTIC_CONTRACT"
0660: AUTH_SCHEMA = "WP004_REAL_EXECUTION_AUTHORIZATION_V1"
0661: BOUNDARY_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA = "WP004_EXTERNAL_BOUNDARY_EVIDENCE_V1"
0662: RISK_SCOPE = "DEVELOPMENT_DEFAULTS_UNCHANGED"
0663: CNC_STATE = "DISABLED"
0664: PRETRAIN_POLICIES = ("REJECT", "WARMUP_PROVENANCE_NO_FIT")
0665: COMMITMENT_SCOPE = "CURRENT_ACQUISITION_IDENTITY_NOT_HISTORICAL_SEALED_MEMBERSHIP"
0666: TRAIN_SCOPE_DISCLOSURE = (
0667:     "retained rows are TRAIN-dated by timestamp filter only; physical bytes are classified solely by the authorized "
0668:     "interval kind (HEADER / TRAIN_DATA / PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT), never inferred from timestamps")
0669: 
0670: # What an independent party must supply per file; the harness validates presence/shape only. A bounded extractor
0671: # (this harness included) cannot create trusted boundary evidence retroactively: producing it requires reading the bytes.
0672: REQUIRED_EXTERNAL_BOUNDARY_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA = {
0673:     "schema": BOUNDARY_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA,
0674:     "evidence_id": "non-empty string identifying the external audit record",
0675:     "evidence_artifact_sha256": "sha256 of the external audit artefact (64 lowercase hex)",
0676:     "issuer": "non-empty string naming the independent issuer",
0677:     "independent_of_harness": "true (self-asserted here; trust is established outside this harness)",
0678:     "file_size_bytes_at_audit": "positive integer; must equal the current stat size of the file",
0679: }
0680: _AUTH_KEYS = {"schema", "execution_package_id", "contract_id", "contract_sha256", "classification",
0681:               "authorizes_real_data_read", "pins", "roots", "stage_b", "risk_scope", "cnc_runtime", "pretrain_policy",
0682:               "commitment_scope", "files"}
0683: _PIN_KEYS = {"snapshot_manifest_sha256", "protocol_sha256", "params_sha256", "snapshot_head"}
0684: _ROOT_KEYS = {"manifest_root", "data_root", "grid_root", "output_root", "output_dir"}
0685: _FILE_KEYS = {"role", "name", "canonical_path", "intervals", "max_physical_bytes", "content_commitment_sha256",
0686:               "provenance_id", "boundary_evidence", "terminal_boundary_row_authorized"}
0687: _EVIDENCE_KEYS = set(REQUIRED_EXTERNAL_BOUNDARY_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA)
0688: 
0689: 
0690: class UnplannedFileError(ReadBoundaryError):
0691:     pass
0692: 
0693: 
0694: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0695: class FilePlan:
0696:     role: str
0697:     name: str
0698:     path: str
0699:     intervals: tuple
0700:     max_physical_bytes: int
0701:     content_commitment_sha256: str
0702:     provenance_id: str
0703:     boundary_evidence: dict
0704:     terminal_boundary_row_authorized: bool
0705: 
0706:     @property
0707:     def planned_bytes(self) -> int:
0708:         return sum(end - start for start, end, _ in self.intervals)
0709: 
0710: 
0711: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0712: class ReadPlan:
0713:     authorization_sha256: str
0714:     execution_package_id: str
0715:     pretrain_policy: str
0716:     files: dict   # canonical path -> FilePlan
0717: 
0718:     def require(self, path) -> FilePlan:
0719:         entry = self.files.get(str(path))
0720:         if entry is None:
0721:             raise UnplannedFileError(f"{path}: not in the authorized read plan")
0722:         return entry
0723: 
0724:     def check_coverage(self, actual: dict):
0725:         """actual: canonical path -> (role, name, size_bytes). Exact bijection with the plan; sizes bound the intervals."""
0726:         if set(actual) != set(self.files):
0727:             raise AuthorizationError(
0728:                 f"read plan files differ from metadata plan: missing={sorted(set(actual) - set(self.files))[:5]} "
0729:                 f"unexpected={sorted(set(self.files) - set(actual))[:5]}")
0730:         for path, (role, name, size) in actual.items():
0731:             entry = self.files[path]
0732:             if (entry.role, entry.name) != (role, name):
0733:                 raise AuthorizationError(f"{path}: plan role/name {(entry.role, entry.name)} != {(role, name)}")
0734:             if entry.intervals[-1][1] >= size:
0735:                 raise AuthorizationError(f"{path}: authorized interval does not end strictly before file size {size}")
0736:             if entry.boundary_evidence["file_size_bytes_at_audit"] != size:
0737:                 raise AuthorizationError(f"{path}: file size differs from boundary evidence size")
0738: 
0739:     def receipt(self) -> dict:
0740:         return dict(
0741:             authorization_sha256=self.authorization_sha256, execution_package_id=self.execution_package_id,
0742:             pretrain_policy=self.pretrain_policy, commitment_scope=COMMITMENT_SCOPE,
0743:             commitment_disclosure="a freshly supplied prefix commitment is the CURRENT ACQUISITION identity of the consumed "
0744:                                   "bytes; it is not historical sealed membership and is not independent validation",
0745:             train_scope=TRAIN_SCOPE_DISCLOSURE,
0746:             files=[dict(role=f.role, name=f.name, path=f.path, intervals=[list(i) for i in f.intervals],
0747:                         max_physical_bytes=f.max_physical_bytes, provenance_id=f.provenance_id,
0748:                         content_commitment_sha256=f.content_commitment_sha256,
0749:                         boundary_evidence=dict(f.boundary_evidence),
0750:                         terminal_boundary_row_authorized=f.terminal_boundary_row_authorized)
0751:                    for _, f in sorted(self.files.items())])
0752: 
0753: 
0754: def _exact_keys(obj, keys, label):
0755:     if not isinstance(obj, dict) or set(obj) != set(keys):
0756:         extra = sorted(set(obj) - set(keys)) if isinstance(obj, dict) else "not an object"
0757:         missing = sorted(set(keys) - set(obj)) if isinstance(obj, dict) else "-"
0758:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{label}: keys must be exactly {sorted(keys)} (extra={extra} missing={missing})")
0759:     return obj
0760: 
0761: 
0762: def _canon_dir(value, label):
0763:     if not isinstance(value, str) or not Path(value).is_absolute() or ".." in Path(value).parts:
0764:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{label}: must be an absolute path without '..'")
0765:     return str(Path(value).resolve())
0766: 
0767: 
0768: def _parse_file_entry(entry, pretrain_policy, roots_by_role, feed_names):
0769:     _exact_keys(entry, _FILE_KEYS, "file entry")
0770:     role, name, path = entry["role"], entry["name"], entry["canonical_path"]
0771:     if role not in ("stock", "grid") or not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
0772:         raise AuthorizationError(f"file entry: bad role/name {role!r}/{name!r}")
0773:     if role == "grid" and name not in feed_names:
0774:         raise AuthorizationError(f"grid feed {name!r} not in protocol")
0775:     if not isinstance(path, str) or not Path(path).is_absolute() or ".." in Path(path).parts or str(Path(path).resolve()) != path:
0776:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: canonical_path must be an absolute, already-resolved path")
0777:     if not Path(path).is_relative_to(roots_by_role[role]):
0778:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: canonical_path outside the declared {role} root")
0779:     raw = entry["intervals"]
0780:     if not isinstance(raw, list) or not raw:
0781:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: intervals must be a non-empty list")
0782:     parsed = []
0783:     for item in raw:
0784:         _exact_keys(item, {"start", "end", "kind"}, f"{name}: interval")
0785:         parsed.append((item["start"], item["end"], item["kind"]))
0786:     try:
0787:         intervals = normalize_intervals(parsed)
0788:     except ReadBoundaryError as exc:
0789:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: {exc}") from None
0790:     kinds = [k for _, _, k in intervals]
0791:     if intervals[0][0] != 0 or kinds[0] != "HEADER" or kinds.count("HEADER") != 1:
0792:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: first (and only) HEADER interval must start at byte 0")
0793:     if "PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT" in kinds:
0794:         if pretrain_policy != "WARMUP_PROVENANCE_NO_FIT":
0795:             raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: preTRAIN interval present but pretrain_policy is {pretrain_policy}")
0796:         if role != "stock":
0797:             raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: preTRAIN rows are never authorized for grid feeds")
0798:     if "TRAIN_DATA" not in kinds:
0799:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: no TRAIN_DATA interval")
0800:     if "PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT" in kinds \
0801:             and kinds.index("TRAIN_DATA") < len(kinds) - 1 - kinds[::-1].index("PRETRAIN_WARMUP_NO_FIT"):
0802:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: preTRAIN intervals must precede TRAIN_DATA intervals")
0803:     try:
0804:         limit = _positive_int(entry["max_physical_bytes"], f"{name}: max_physical_bytes")
0805:     except HarnessError as exc:
0806:         raise AuthorizationError(str(exc)) from None
0807:     if limit != sum(end - start for start, end, _ in intervals):
0808:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: max_physical_bytes must equal the total authorized interval bytes")
0809:     if not is_sha256(entry["content_commitment_sha256"]):
0810:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: malformed content_commitment_sha256")
0811:     if not isinstance(entry["provenance_id"], str) or not entry["provenance_id"].strip():
0812:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: provenance_id missing")
0813:     evidence = _exact_keys(entry["boundary_evidence"], _EVIDENCE_KEYS, f"{name}: boundary_evidence")
0814:     if (evidence["schema"] != BOUNDARY_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA or evidence["independent_of_harness"] is not True
0815:             or not is_sha256(evidence["evidence_artifact_sha256"])
0816:             or not all(isinstance(evidence[k], str) and evidence[k].strip() for k in ("evidence_id", "issuer"))):
0817:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: boundary evidence incomplete/invalid (required schema: "
0818:                                  f"{json.dumps(REQUIRED_EXTERNAL_BOUNDARY_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA)})")
0819:     try:
0820:         _positive_int(evidence["file_size_bytes_at_audit"], f"{name}: file_size_bytes_at_audit")
0821:     except HarnessError as exc:
0822:         raise AuthorizationError(str(exc)) from None
0823:     if entry["terminal_boundary_row_authorized"] is not True:
0824:         raise AuthorizationError(f"{name}: terminal boundary row (sentinel / cutoff row) must be explicitly authorized")
0825:     return FilePlan(role=role, name=name, path=path, intervals=intervals, max_physical_bytes=limit,
0826:                     content_commitment_sha256=entry["content_commitment_sha256"], provenance_id=entry["provenance_id"],
0827:                     boundary_evidence=dict(evidence), terminal_boundary_row_authorized=True)
0828: 
0829: 
0830: def validate_real_authorization(auth_bytes: bytes, *, authorization_sha256_pin, protocol, bindings) -> ReadPlan:
0831:     """Pure validation of the entire authorization against independently supplied bindings. Opens NO data file.
0832: 
0833:     ``bindings`` (all supplied by the caller, never read from the authorization): contract_sha256, snapshot_manifest_sha256,
0834:     protocol_sha256, params_sha256, manifest_root, data_root, grid_root, output_root, output_dir.
0835:     Anything missing, unknown, or different refuses; there is no override key.
0836:     """
0837:     if not is_sha256(authorization_sha256_pin):
0838:         raise AuthorizationError("malformed authorization sha256 pin")
0839:     actual_sha = sha256_bytes(auth_bytes)
0840:     if actual_sha != authorization_sha256_pin:
0841:         raise AuthorizationError("authorization file sha256 differs from pin")
0842:     try:
0843:         auth = json.loads(auth_bytes)
0844:     except ValueError as exc:
0845:         raise AuthorizationError(f"authorization is not valid JSON: {exc}") from None
0846:     _exact_keys(auth, _AUTH_KEYS, "authorization")
0847:     expected = {"schema": AUTH_SCHEMA, "contract_id": CONTRACT_ID, "classification": CLASSIFICATION,
0848:                 "authorizes_real_data_read": True, "risk_scope": RISK_SCOPE, "cnc_runtime": CNC_STATE,
0849:                 "commitment_scope": COMMITMENT_SCOPE}
0850:     for key, value in expected.items():
0851:         if auth[key] != value or type(auth[key]) is not type(value):
0852:             raise AuthorizationError(f"authorization {key} != required {value!r}")
0853:     if not isinstance(auth["execution_package_id"], str) or not auth["execution_package_id"].strip():
0854:         raise AuthorizationError("execution_package_id missing")
0855:     if auth["contract_sha256"] != bindings["contract_sha256"] or not is_sha256(auth["contract_sha256"]):
0856:         raise AuthorizationError("contract_sha256 differs from the supplied contract file")
0857:     if auth["pretrain_policy"] not in PRETRAIN_POLICIES:
0858:         raise AuthorizationError("pretrain_policy must be REJECT or WARMUP_PROVENANCE_NO_FIT")
0859:     pins = _exact_keys(auth["pins"], _PIN_KEYS, "pins")
0860:     for key in ("snapshot_manifest_sha256", "protocol_sha256", "params_sha256"):
0861:         if not is_sha256(pins[key]) or pins[key] != bindings[key]:
0862:             raise AuthorizationError(f"pin {key} differs from supplied value")
0863:     if pins["snapshot_head"] != EXPECTED_HEAD:
0864:         raise AuthorizationError("snapshot_head differs from harness EXPECTED_HEAD")
0865:     roots = _exact_keys(auth["roots"], _ROOT_KEYS, "roots")
0866:     resolved = {k: _canon_dir(roots[k], f"roots.{k}") for k in _ROOT_KEYS}
0867:     for key in _ROOT_KEYS:
0868:         if resolved[key] != _canon_dir(str(bindings[key]), f"binding {key}"):
0869:             raise AuthorizationError(f"root {key} differs from supplied value")
0870:     stage = _exact_keys(auth["stage_b"], {"block", "sessions"}, "stage_b")
0871:     declared = protocol["sampling_plan"]["stage_b"][0]
0872:     if stage["block"] != 1 or type(stage["block"]) is not int or int(declared["block"]) != 1 \
0873:             or stage["sessions"] != list(declared["sessions"]):
0874:         raise AuthorizationError("stage_b block/sessions differ from protocol Stage B Block 1")
0875:     entries = auth["files"]
0876:     if not isinstance(entries, list) or not entries:
0877:         raise AuthorizationError("files must be a non-empty list")
0878:     roles = {"stock": Path(resolved["data_root"]), "grid": Path(resolved["grid_root"])}
0879:     plan = {}
0880:     for entry in entries:
0881:         fp = _parse_file_entry(entry, auth["pretrain_policy"], roles, set(protocol["grid_dataset"]["feeds"]))
0882:         if fp.path in plan:
0883:             raise AuthorizationError(f"duplicate file entry {fp.path}")
0884:         plan[fp.path] = fp
0885:     return ReadPlan(authorization_sha256=actual_sha, execution_package_id=auth["execution_package_id"],
0886:                     pretrain_policy=auth["pretrain_policy"], files=plan)
0887: 
0888: 
0889: OS_ISOLATION_WITNESS_IMPLEMENTED = False   # stays False: the bwrap launcher exists (os_isolation.py) but real-data release is closed
0890: 
0891: 
0892: def os_isolation_witness() -> dict:
0893:     """Gate for REAL execution (WP-R5-004-OS-ISOLATION). Two conditions, both code, neither satisfiable from JSON:
0894: 
0895:     1. the process runs inside a bwrap boundary that ``os_isolation.launch`` created and the bootstrap verified; the live
0896:        kernel state (namespaces, mounts, root listing, fds, capabilities, interfaces) is re-probed on every call;
0897:     2. the real-data release is open (``os_isolation.require_real_run_release``). It is CLOSED: no trusted TRAIN-only input
0898:        artifacts / Director release acceptance exist, so this refuses even inside a verified sandbox.
0899:     Python tripwires (builtins.open/io.open/sockets/class patches) do NOT see os.open, mmap, native libraries or other
0900:     processes and are not evidence of IO restriction."""
0901:     try:
0902:         import os_isolation as osi
0903:     except ImportError:
0904:         raise IsolationGateError("real execution refused: OS-level IO isolation launcher unavailable (fails closed)") from None
0905:     try:
0906:         witness = osi.active_witness()
0907:     except osi.OSIsolationError as exc:
0908:         raise IsolationGateError(f"real execution refused: no verified OS-level IO isolation witness ({exc}); Python "
0909:                                  "tripwires do not prove native/system IO restriction") from None
0910:     try:
0911:         osi.require_real_run_release(witness)
0912:     except osi.OSIsolationError as exc:
0913:         raise IsolationGateError(f"real execution refused: OS-level boundary verified but {exc}") from None
0914:     raise IsolationGateError("real execution refused: release path ended without an explicit opening (fails closed)")
0915: 
0916: 
0917: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0918: class HarnessConfig:
0919:     snapshot_dir: Path
0920:     allowed_manifest_roots: tuple
0921:     allowed_data_roots: tuple
0922:     allowed_grid_roots: tuple
0923:     output_root: Path
0924:     chunk_size: int = DEFAULT_CHUNK
0925:     max_physical_bytes_per_file: int | None = None
0926:     strict_prefix: bool = True
0927:     bind_stage_b_block1: bool = True
0928:     read_plan: ReadPlan | None = None
0929:     real_execution: bool = False
0930:     pretrain_policy: str = "REJECT"
0931: 
0932:     def __post_init__(self):
0933:         _positive_int(self.chunk_size, "chunk_size")
0934:         if self.max_physical_bytes_per_file is not None:
0935:             _positive_int(self.max_physical_bytes_per_file, "max_physical_bytes_per_file")
0936:         if self.pretrain_policy not in PRETRAIN_POLICIES:
0937:             raise HarnessError(f"pretrain_policy must be one of {PRETRAIN_POLICIES}")
0938:         if self.real_execution and self.read_plan is None:
0939:             raise AuthorizationError("real execution requires an authorized per-file read plan (fails closed)")
0940:         if self.read_plan is not None and self.read_plan.pretrain_policy != self.pretrain_policy:
0941:             raise AuthorizationError("config pretrain_policy differs from authorization")

0943: 
0944: def recompute_manifest_hash(manifest) -> str:
0945:     payload = json.dumps([asdict(r) for r in manifest.files], sort_keys=True, default=str)
0946:     return sha256_bytes(payload.encode("utf-8"))
0947: 
0948: 
0949: def validate_block(protocol, block, config):
0950:     import pandas as pd
0951:     sessions = list(block["sessions"])
0952:     if int(block["block"]) != 1:
0953:         raise HarnessError("Stage B Block 1 only")
0954:     parsed = [pd.Timestamp(s).date() for s in sessions]
0955:     if not sessions or parsed != sorted(set(parsed)):
0956:         raise ChronologyError("block sessions must be unique and ascending")
0957:     start, end = _train_bounds(protocol)
0958:     if parsed[0] < start or parsed[-1] >= end:
0959:         raise ChronologyError("block sessions outside TRAIN interval")
0960:     if config.bind_stage_b_block1:
0961:         declared = protocol["sampling_plan"]["stage_b"][0]
0962:         if int(declared["block"]) != 1 or list(declared["sessions"]) != sessions:
0963:             raise HarnessError("block differs from protocol Stage B Block 1")
0964:     engine = protocol["engine"]
0965:     if engine.get("live_trading") is not False or engine.get("broker_network_access") is not False:
0966:         raise HarnessError("protocol engine must declare live_trading=false and broker_network_access=false")
0967:     return sessions
0968: 
0969: 
0970: def make_prepare(config: HarnessConfig, ledger: ReadLedger):
0971:     """Return a prepare(root, protocol, block) -> (frames, feeds, audit) drop-in for worker.prepare_block."""
0972:     manifest_roots = _resolve_roots(config.allowed_manifest_roots, "manifest")
0973:     data_roots = _resolve_roots(config.allowed_data_roots, "data")
0974:     grid_roots = _resolve_roots(config.allowed_grid_roots, "grid")
0975: 
0976:     def prepare(root, protocol, block):
0977:         import pandas as pd
0978:         w = _worker()
0979:         from revision2.dataset_manifest import DatasetManifest
0980:         from revision2_external.orchestrator import Revision2ExternalEngineOrchestrator
0981:         sessions = validate_block(protocol, block, config)
0982:         root = Path(root)
0983:         if not root.is_absolute() or ".." in root.parts or not root.resolve().is_dir() or not _inside(root.resolve(), manifest_roots):
0984:             raise UnsafeRootError(f"unsafe root {root}")
0985:         rel = Path(protocol["stock_dataset"]["manifest"])
0986:         if rel.is_absolute() or ".." in rel.parts:
0987:             raise UnsafeRootError("manifest path must be root-relative")
0988:         manifest_path = _safe_file(root.resolve() / rel, (root.resolve(),), "stock manifest")
0989:         manifest = DatasetManifest.load(str(manifest_path))
0990:         pinned = protocol["stock_dataset"]["manifest_hash"]
0991:         if not is_sha256(pinned) or not is_sha256(manifest.manifest_hash):
0992:             raise ManifestMismatchError("malformed manifest hash")
0993:         if manifest.manifest_hash != pinned:
0994:             raise ValueError("stock manifest identity mismatch")
0995:         if recompute_manifest_hash(manifest) != pinned:
0996:             raise ManifestMismatchError("manifest records do not reproduce pinned manifest hash")
0997:         symbols = [r.symbol for r in manifest.files]
0998:         if len(set(symbols)) != len(symbols) or manifest.symbol_count != len(symbols):
0999:             raise ManifestMismatchError("manifest symbol set inconsistent")
1000:         if "symbols" in protocol["stock_dataset"] and len(symbols) != int(protocol["stock_dataset"]["symbols"]):
1001:             raise ManifestMismatchError("manifest symbol count differs from protocol")
1002:         data_dir = Path(manifest.data_dir)
1003:         if not data_dir.is_absolute() or ".." in data_dir.parts or not data_dir.resolve().is_dir() \
1004:                 or not _inside(data_dir.resolve(), data_roots):
1005:             raise UnsafeRootError(f"manifest data_dir outside declared data roots: {data_dir}")
1006:         data_dir = data_dir.resolve()
1007:         warmup_bars = int(protocol["block_execution_contract"]["stock_warmup_bars_per_symbol"])
1008:         end = protocol["periods"]["train_end_exclusive"]
1009:         train_start, _ = _train_bounds(protocol)
1010:         frames, stock_audit = {}, {}
1011:         # ---- PREFLIGHT: all metadata, paths, sizes and the read plan are validated before ANY data file is opened
1012:         stock_files = []
1013:         for record in sorted(manifest.files, key=lambda r: r.symbol):
1014:             if not is_sha256(record.sha256):
1015:                 raise ManifestMismatchError(f"{record.symbol}: malformed record hash")
1016:             name = record.filename
1017:             if Path(name).name != name or not name.endswith(".csv") or name.startswith("."):
1018:                 raise UnsafeRootError(f"{record.symbol}: unsafe filename {name!r}")
1019:             path = _safe_file(data_dir / name, (data_dir,), record.symbol)
1020:             if path.stat().st_size != record.size_bytes:
1021:                 raise ManifestMismatchError(f"{record.symbol}: file size differs from manifest")
1022:             stock_files.append((record, path))
1023:         grid_rel = Path(protocol["grid_dataset"]["local_manifest"])
1024:         if grid_rel.is_absolute() or ".." in grid_rel.parts:
1025:             raise UnsafeRootError("grid manifest path must be root-relative")
1026:         grid_manifest = _safe_file(root.resolve() / grid_rel, (root.resolve(),), "grid manifest")
1027:         entries = json.loads(grid_manifest.read_text())["files"]
1028:         names = [e["name"] for e in entries]
1029:         if len(set(names)) != len(names) or set(names) != set(protocol["grid_dataset"]["feeds"]):
1030:             raise ValueError("grid feed set differs from sealed protocol")
1031:         grid_records = {e["name"]: e for e in entries}
1032:         grid_paths = {}
1033:         for gname in sorted(grid_records):
1034:             rec = grid_records[gname]
1035:             sealed_sha = protocol["grid_dataset"]["feeds"][gname].get("sha256")
1036:             if not is_sha256(sealed_sha) or not is_sha256(rec.get("sha256")):
1037:                 raise ManifestMismatchError(f"{gname}: malformed grid sha256")
1038:             if rec["sha256"] != sealed_sha:
1039:                 raise ValueError(f"{gname}: local manifest SHA mismatch")
1040:             grid_paths[gname] = _safe_file(Path(rec["path"]), grid_roots, f"grid feed {gname}")
1041:         plan = config.read_plan
1042:         if config.real_execution and plan is None:
1043:             raise AuthorizationError("real execution requires an authorized per-file read plan")
1044:         if plan is not None:
1045:             actual = {str(path): ("stock", record.symbol, path.stat().st_size) for record, path in stock_files}
1046:             actual.update({str(path): ("grid", gname, path.stat().st_size) for gname, path in grid_paths.items()})
1047:             plan.check_coverage(actual)
1048:         # ---- end preflight: first data open happens below
1049:         for record, path in stock_files:
1050:             frame, audit, row = load_stock_block_bounded(
1051:                 path, sessions, warmup_bars, train_end_exclusive=end, record=record, chunk_size=config.chunk_size,
1052:                 max_physical_bytes=config.max_physical_bytes_per_file, strict_prefix=config.strict_prefix,
1053:                 file_plan=None if plan is None else plan.require(path), train_start=train_start,
1054:                 pretrain_policy=config.pretrain_policy)
1055:             ledger.files.append(dict(row, role="stock", symbol=record.symbol,
1056:                                      provenance_id=None if plan is None else plan.require(path).provenance_id))
1057:             frames[record.symbol] = frame
1058:             stock_audit[record.symbol] = audit
1059:         certified = Revision2ExternalEngineOrchestrator.prepare_market_data(frames)
1060:         clock = Revision2ExternalEngineOrchestrator.build_clock(certified, warmup_bars)
1061:         if len(clock) != sum(a["target_rows"] for a in stock_audit.values()):
1062:             raise ValueError(f"clock event mismatch: {len(clock)}")
1063:         target_dates = {pd.Timestamp(x).date() for x in sessions}
1064:         for event in clock:
1065:             day = w.exchange_ts(event.timestamp).date()
1066:             if day not in target_dates:
1067:                 raise ValueError(f"clock escaped target sessions: {day}")
1068:             if event.bar_idx >= len(certified[event.symbol]) - 1:
1069:                 raise ValueError("boundary sentinel entered trading clock")
1070:         max_target_utc = max(w.exchange_ts(f["timestamp"].iloc[-2]).tz_convert("UTC") for f in certified.values())
1071:         feeds, grid_audit, grid_rows = load_grid_prefix_bounded(
1072:             grid_records, protocol, max_target_utc, allowed_grid_roots=grid_roots,
1073:             chunk_size=config.chunk_size, max_physical_bytes=config.max_physical_bytes_per_file,
1074:             strict_prefix=config.strict_prefix, read_plan=plan, train_start=train_start)
1075:         for row in grid_rows:
1076:             ledger.files.append(dict(row, role="grid",
1077:                                      provenance_id=None if plan is None else plan.require(row["path"]).provenance_id))
1078:         canonical = {
1079:             "loader": LOADER_ID, "classification": CLASSIFICATION, "block": int(block["block"]), "sessions": sessions,
1080:             "warmup_bars_per_symbol": warmup_bars, "stock": stock_audit, "clock_events": len(clock), "grid": grid_audit,
1081:             "max_target_utc": max_target_utc.isoformat(),
1082:         }
1083:         canonical["slice_sha256"] = w.canonical_json_hash(canonical)
1084:         return certified, feeds, canonical
1085: 
1086:     return prepare

1091:     ("kiteconnect", "KiteConnect.__init__"),
1092:     ("revision2_external.broker_adapter_kite", "KiteConnectBrokerAdapter.__init__"),
1093:     ("revision2_external.broker_adapter_kite", "KiteConnectBrokerAdapter.get_gtts"),

1097:     ("revision2_external.instrument_metadata", "InstrumentMetadataSnapshot.build"),

1104: )
1105: _WRITE_MODES = set("wax+")
1106: _MISSING = object()
1107: 
1108: 
1109: class Tripwire:
1110:     """Counting guard: any CNC/GTT/tick/conversion/network/raw-market-file access raises and is recorded."""
1111: 
1112:     def __init__(self, forbidden_read_roots=(), allowed_write_roots=(), extra_targets=(), planned_paths=None):
1113:         self.planned_paths = None if planned_paths is None else frozenset(str(p) for p in planned_paths)
1114:         self.counts = {}
1115:         self.violations = []
1116:         self.forbidden_read_roots = tuple(Path(p).resolve() for p in forbidden_read_roots)
1117:         self.allowed_write_roots = tuple(Path(p).resolve() for p in allowed_write_roots)
1118:         self.targets = TRIPWIRE_TARGETS + tuple(extra_targets)
1119:         self._restore = []
1120: 
1121:     def _trip(self, name):
1122:         self.counts[name] = self.counts.get(name, 0) + 1
1123:         self.violations.append(name)
1124:         raise TripwireViolation(f"forbidden access: {name}")
1125: 
1126:     def _patch(self, owner, attr, label):
1127:         original = owner.__dict__.get(attr, _MISSING) if isinstance(owner, type) else getattr(owner, attr)
1128:         self._restore.append((owner, attr, original))
1129: 
1130:         def tripped(*args, **kwargs):
1131:             self._trip(label)
1132:         setattr(owner, attr, tripped)
1133: 
1134:     def __enter__(self):
1135:         for module_name, dotted in self.targets:
1136:             module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
1137:             parts = dotted.split(".")
1138:             owner = module if len(parts) == 1 else getattr(module, parts[0])
1139:             self._patch(owner, parts[-1], f"{module_name}:{dotted}")
1140:         for owner, attr in ((socket.socket, "connect"), (socket.socket, "connect_ex"),
1141:                             (socket, "create_connection"), (socket, "getaddrinfo")):
1142:             self._patch(owner, attr, f"socket.{attr}")
1143:         original_open = builtins.open
1144:         self._restore.append((builtins, "open", original_open))
1145:         guard = self
1146: 
1147:         def guarded_open(file, mode="r", *args, **kwargs):
1148:             if (isinstance(file, (str, bytes, os.PathLike)) and getattr(_IN_HARNESS_READ, "active", False)
1149:                     and guard.planned_paths is not None
1150:                     and str(Path(os.fsdecode(file)).resolve()) not in guard.planned_paths):
1151:                 guard._trip(f"unplanned_harness_open:{file}")
1152:             if isinstance(file, (str, bytes, os.PathLike)) and not getattr(_IN_HARNESS_READ, "active", False):
1153:                 resolved = Path(os.fsdecode(file)).resolve()
1154:                 if any(resolved == r or resolved.is_relative_to(r) for r in guard.forbidden_read_roots):
1155:                     guard._trip(f"raw_open:{resolved}")
1156:                 if _WRITE_MODES & set(mode) and not any(resolved.is_relative_to(r) for r in guard.allowed_write_roots):
1157:                     guard._trip(f"write_outside_output:{resolved}")
1158:             return original_open(file, mode, *args, **kwargs)
1159:         self._restore.append((io, "open", original_open))
1160:         builtins.open = guarded_open
1161:         io.open = guarded_open
1162:         return self
1163: 
1164:     def __exit__(self, *exc):
1165:         for owner, attr, original in reversed(self._restore):
1166:             if original is _MISSING:
1167:                 delattr(owner, attr)
1168:             else:
1169:                 setattr(owner, attr, original)
1170:         self._restore.clear()
1171:         return False
1172: 
1173:     def report(self):
1174:         return dict(counts=dict(self.counts), violations=list(self.violations), targets=[f"{m}:{d}" for m, d in self.targets],
1175:                     zero_access=not self.violations,
1176:                     kiteconnect_module_loaded_import_only="kiteconnect" in sys.modules)
1177: 
1178: 
1179: # --------------------------------------------------------------- execution
1180: def check_output_path(output_path, config):
1181:     out = Path(output_path)
1182:     if not out.is_absolute() or ".." in out.parts:
1183:         raise UnsafeRootError("output path must be absolute without '..'")
1184:     resolved = out.resolve()
1185:     root = Path(config.output_root).resolve()
1186:     if resolved == root or not resolved.is_relative_to(root):
1187:         raise UnsafeRootError("output outside isolated output root")
1188:     protected = [Path(config.snapshot_dir).resolve(), *map(Path, FORBIDDEN_REPOS),
1189:                  *_resolve_roots(config.allowed_data_roots, "data"), *_resolve_roots(config.allowed_grid_roots, "grid")]
1190:     if any(_inside(resolved, [p.resolve()]) for p in protected) or _inside(root, [Path(p).resolve() for p in FORBIDDEN_REPOS]):
1191:         raise UnsafeRootError("output overlaps protected tree")
1192:     if resolved.exists() and (not resolved.is_dir() or any(resolved.iterdir())):
1193:         raise UnsafeRootError("output directory must not pre-exist with content")
1194:     return resolved

1197: def assert_factory_contract(factory):
1198:     """Fail closed if the snapshot factory does not expose the direct explicit-prepare route."""
1199:     import inspect
1200:     params = inspect.signature(factory.execute_block).parameters
1201:     needed = ("combined_cycle_state_path", "output_path", "fleet_loading_policy", "feedback_compaction", "prepare")
1202:     missing = [n for n in needed if n not in params]
1203:     if missing:
1204:         raise HarnessError(f"direct factory lacks required parameters {missing}; refusing to bypass")

1206: 
1207: def run_block_diagnostic(config: HarnessConfig, root, protocol, block, params, output_path, *,
1208:                          expected_manifest_sha256=None):
1209:     """Direct-factory execution: combined_cycle_state_path=None, fleet policy None, feedback_compaction False."""
1210:     if config.real_execution:
1211:         if config.read_plan is None:
1212:             raise AuthorizationError("real execution requires an authorized per-file read plan")
1213:         os_witness = os_isolation_witness()      # refuses (IsolationGateError) until external OS-level evidence exists
1214:     else:
1215:         os_witness = None
1216:     identity = verify_snapshot(config.snapshot_dir, expected_manifest_sha256=expected_manifest_sha256)
1217:     activate_snapshot(config.snapshot_dir)
1218:     out = check_output_path(output_path, config)
1219:     factory = importlib.import_module("scripts.diagnostics.r5_block1_acceptance")
1220:     assert_factory_contract(factory)
1221:     ledger = ReadLedger()
1222:     prepare = make_prepare(config, ledger)
1223:     forbidden = [*_resolve_roots(config.allowed_data_roots, "data"), *_resolve_roots(config.allowed_grid_roots, "grid")]
1224:     planned = None if config.read_plan is None else config.read_plan.files
1225:     with Tripwire(forbidden_read_roots=forbidden, allowed_write_roots=[out], planned_paths=planned) as tripwire:
1226:         result = factory.execute_block(
1227:             Path(root), protocol, block, params, combined_cycle_state_path=None, output_path=out,
1228:             fleet_loading_policy=None, feedback_compaction=False, prepare=prepare)
1229:     post = verify_snapshot(config.snapshot_dir, expected_manifest_sha256=expected_manifest_sha256)
1230:     if post["tree_sha256"] != identity["tree_sha256"]:
1231:         raise CodeDriftError("snapshot changed during execution")
1232:     receipt = dict(
1233:         classification=CLASSIFICATION, claims="none: mechanics only; not sealed, not actual48_certified, not performance",
1234:         snapshot={k: v for k, v in identity.items() if k != "files"},
1235:         imported_sources=audit_loaded_modules(config.snapshot_dir, identity["files"]),
1236:         environment=environment_fingerprint(), read_accounting=ledger.summary(), read_files=ledger.files,
1237:         tripwire=tripwire.report(), combined_cycle_runtime=None, route=dict(
1238:             factory="scripts/diagnostics/r5_block1_acceptance.py:execute_block", combined_cycle_state_path=None,
1239:             fleet_loading_policy=None, feedback_compaction=False, prepare="harness.make_prepare"),
1240:         real_execution=config.real_execution, os_isolation_witness=os_witness,
1241:         python_tripwire_limits="in-process Python patches only; they do not prove native/system IO restriction",
1242:         authorization=None if config.read_plan is None else config.read_plan.receipt(),
1243:         block_fingerprint=result.get("block_fingerprint"), slice_sha256=result["audit"]["slice_sha256"])
1244:     (out / "harness_receipt.json").write_text(json.dumps(receipt, sort_keys=True, indent=2, default=str, allow_nan=False) + "\n")
1245:     return result, receipt
